5.6 Pitfalls

Pitfall Manifestation Root cause Debug signal
Top-right substitution wrong diagonal-down-left artifacts at right MB edges using unavailable top-right instead of replicated sample visual checkerboard at right edge
DC partial mean rounding +/-1 luma value mismatch vs JM integer division rounding (should be (sum + n/2) / n) unit test delta = 1 on partial-neighbor cases
Plane coefficients overflow wrap-around pixels at high contrast H+V sums not widened to i32 before scaling pixel values > 255 or < 0 before clip
Constrained intra ignored encoder uses inter-coded neighbors for prediction missing flag propagation from slice header encoder/decoder mismatch on constrained streams
MPM derivation off-by-one; neighbor buffer stale excess mode bits in bitstream; wrong prediction at row start rem_mode encodes mode-1 when > MPM, mode when <; left-neighbor buffer not cleared between rows bitstream larger than expected, compare to JM; first MB of each row looks wrong

5.7 Performance Concerns

  • predict_intra4x4 called up to 16 times per MB (one per 4x4 block); mode decision tries all 9 modes per block: 16 × 9 = 144 prediction evaluations per MB luma — keep each prediction O(16).
  • Neighbor sample fetch is a memcpy from reconstructed plane; cache-friendly if plane is row-major and MB processed in raster order.
  • Plane mode: precompute H and V once per block (8 multiply-adds each; 32 for 16x16 plane) — still negligible vs motion search.

5.8 Edge Cases

Edge case Handling
First MB (top-left corner); single-MB frame top, left, top-left all unavailable; DC → 128
Left edge MB; top edge MB left unavailable → vertical uses top only; top unavailable → horizontal uses left only
Slice boundary treat first MB in slice as left-neighbor unavailable if deblocking_filter_control=1 doesn't cross
Top-right neighbor last in row replicate top[3] for positions top_right[0..3]
Chroma plane with one quadrant available DC uses only available quadrant mean for that quadrant

5.9 Failure Analysis

Failure Detection Blast radius Recovery
Wrong mode output unit test mismatch vs reference single block; no cascade compare against JM per-pixel output
Availability mask bug corrupted prediction at specific positions every MB at that frame position add per-position availability log
Plane arithmetic overflow; stale neighbor buffer corrupted pixels, possible panic (debug); entire row wrong entire 16x16 block; one row per frame add saturating_add, clip to [0,255]; initialize neighbor buffer at row start

5.10 Operational Notes

  • Log per-frame mode distribution: {dc: N, vert: N, horiz: N, plane: N, diag0..5: N} — feeds P10 observability.
  • No I/O, no allocation in hot path; predict_* functions are pure (no side effects).
  • vc-intra is stateless per call; neighbor buffers owned by encoder/decoder driver (Phase 6).
  • No deployment/migration concern; isolated crate with no external dependencies beyond vc-core.

Phase 6 — Intra-Only Codec (I-Frame End to End)

6.1 Objectives

  • Wire phases 1–5 into a complete intra-only (I-frame) encoder + decoder.
  • Demonstrate bit-exact reconstruction: encode → bitstream → decode → pixels match encoder reconstruction.
  • Produce measurable PSNR for a real YUV input; validate I1, I2, I3, I4.
  • Use placeholder entropy (fixed-length level coding or simple CAVLC subset) — full CAVLC deferred to Phase 7.
  • Establish the reconstruction loop invariant (P2) as a verifiable test.
  • Output: vc-cli encode --intra-only and vc-cli decode commands functional.

6.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

Encoder driver (vc-encoder/src/intra_only.rs) processes every frame macroblock-by-macroblock in raster order; per MB:

  1. extract 16x16 source luma + 8x8 Cb + 8x8 Cr from input Plane.
  2. choose intra mode: try all modes (4x4 or 16x16 decision), pick min-SAD.
  3. predict_intra*() → prediction block, then residual = source - prediction (i16 subtraction, per sample).
  4. forward_dct_4x4() on each 4x4 residual block (16 luma + 8 chroma), then quant_block(qp, intra=true) → levels; collect CBP.
  5. placeholder entropy: write levels as raw i16 words (or simple exp-Golomb per level).
  6. RECONSTRUCTION LOOP (P2): dequant_block() → residual_hat; inverse_dct_4x4() → residual_hat pixels; recon = clip(prediction + residual_hat, 0, 255); store recon in reconstructed Plane (used as neighbor for subsequent MBs).
  7. write MB syntax to bitstream via BitWriter (exp-Golomb mode field + CBP + levels).

16x16 vs 4x4 decision: compare sum_SAD_16x16(mode) to sum_SAD_4x4_best_modes for each MB; pick lower (also try pure 16x16 Plane mode for I-frame quality). Chroma intra: always intra; mode chosen independently by DC/H/V/Plane SAD.

Slice/frame structure: one slice per frame (simplified), one I-slice type; SliceHeader carries slice_type=I, frame_num=0, qp_delta; NAL unit type 5 (IDR slice) wraps the one I-slice.

Decoder driver (vc-decoder/src/intra_only.rs) processes every MB in the same raster order: (1) parse mode field + CBP from BitReader; (2) read levels (same placeholder entropy); (3) dequant_block() → residual_hat, then inverse_dct_4x4(); (4) predict_intra*(mode, decoded_neighbors); (5) recon = clip(prediction + residual, 0, 255); (6) store in output Plane, update neighbor buffer.

Neighbor buffer management: NeighborBuf struct holds a per-row array of top-row reconstructed samples plus per-MB left-column samples, updated in-place after each MB's reconstruction — identical logic in encoder (writes from reconstructed frame) and decoder (writes from decoded frame), guaranteeing I2.

PSNR computation (vc-cli psnr subcommand): psnr(src: &Plane, rec: &Plane) → f64 = 10 * log10(255^2 / MSE); reports per-frame Y-PSNR, U-PSNR, V-PSNR, and frame-average.

Bitstream layout (placeholder format, superseded by Phase 7): slice_header carries ue(qp_delta); per MB: a 1-bit intra_type_flag (0=4x4, 1=16x16), then either 16 × ue(mode_idx) + prev_mode_flag (4x4) or one ue(mode_idx) (16x16), followed by an 8-bit raw cbp, then per nonzero block a ue count plus that many se(level) values.

CLI wiring (vc-cli/src/main.rs): encode --input <yuv> --width W --height H --qp QP --output <bs>; decode --input <bs> --output <yuv>; psnr --src <yuv> --rec <yuv> --width W --height H.

Dependency graph for Phase 6: vc-cli → vc-encoder → vc-intra, vc-transform, vc-quant, vc-bitstream, vc-color, vc-core; vc-cli → vc-decoder → vc-intra, vc-transform, vc-quant, vc-bitstream, vc-core. Anchors: P9 (stage-separated pipeline composition), I1 (deterministic bitstream-to-pixel decode), ADR-03 (13-crate workspace structure enforcing P9), R3 (openh264 slice/MB driver structure, Required Reading Reference Implementations table — applied in this phase).

6.3 Deliverable

  • cargo build --workspace succeeds; all Phase 1–6 crates compile.
  • vc-cli encode --intra-only produces a valid bitstream from a raw YUV file.
  • vc-cli decode reconstructs frames byte-for-byte identical to encoder reconstruction (I1, I2).
  • vc-cli psnr reports Y-PSNR ≥ 30 dB for QP=28 on a standard test sequence (foreman_qcif or similar).
  • Reconstruction-loop identity test: encoder_recon == decoder_output for every pixel.

6.4 Acceptance Criteria

Test Requirement
Bit-exact reconstruction encoder_recon_frame XOR decoder_output_frame == all_zeros for 100-frame test sequence
Y-PSNR floor ≥ 30 dB at QP=28 on 176×144 sequence
Y-PSNR ceiling ≥ 42 dB at QP=10
Bitstream roundtrip parse(serialize(slice_header)) == original
All-intra mode coverage encoder exercises all 9 4x4 modes and all 4 16x16 modes across a diverse frame
Zero drift PSNR of frame N identical whether decoded standalone or after N-1 frames (no DPB drift for I-only)
CLI smoke test encode + decode + psnr pipeline runs on 10-frame QCIF without panic or error

6.5 Conformance Fixtures

Fixture Purpose Expected output
foreman_qcif_10f.yuv reference input (176×144, YUV 4:2:0) source for encode/decode
intra_qp28_recon.yuv encoder reconstruction at QP=28 decoder output must match byte-for-byte
intra_qp28.bs encoded bitstream QP=28 deterministic; re-encoding same input → same bits
intra_qp10_psnr.txt Y/U/V PSNR at QP=10 Y ≥ 42 dB, U/V ≥ 40 dB
intra_qp51_psnr.txt QP=51 extreme quality loss Y ≥ 20 dB (DC prediction survives)
intra_1x1_mb.bs single-macroblock bitstream parse + decode correct
intra_edge_mbs.yuv frame where first/last row and column of MBs tested no availability-mask crash

Malformed: truncated bitstream mid-MB → Err(UnexpectedEof); QP out of range → Err(InvalidQp).

6.6 Pitfalls

Pitfall Manifestation Root cause Debug signal
Encoder uses original pixels as reference drift within same frame; decoder diverges at second MB residual subtracted from source but next MB's left-neighbor taken from source not reconstructed first MB OK, second MB wrong; pixel-diff shows left-edge error
Reconstruction loop skipped for skipped blocks CBP=0 blocks leave residual=0 but prediction still needed branch that skips dequant/idct also skips prediction copy zero-residual MBs decoded as gray
Neighbor buffer misalignment prediction uses wrong left/top samples neighbor written before or after reconstruction systematic ±1 MB horizontal offset in errors
Off-by-one in residual subtraction PSNR lower than expected residual = source - pred should be i16; if done in u8 wraps PSNR varies by frame position, saturation artifacts
Placeholder entropy level range parse failure if level > exp-Golomb table range large residuals at QP=0 with high-frequency content bitstream truncation error at decode
Chroma stride mismatch Cb/Cr corrupted after first row 4:2:0 chroma width = luma_width/2; stride must be halved checkerboard pattern in chroma only
PSNR computation on padded frame inflated PSNR MSE computed over padded rows beyond actual height PSNR varies with padding amount

6.7 Performance Concerns

  • Intra mode decision (16 × 9 SAD evaluations per MB): dominates Phase 6 encode time.
  • SAD inner loop over [u8; 16] — keep branch-free, unroll manually or let compiler unroll.
  • clip(prediction + residual, 0, 255): use u8::saturating_cast from i16; avoids conditional.
  • Neighbor buffer: fits in a few KB for QCIF; entire frame MB-row is hot in L1.
  • Encoding a 176×144 frame = 99 MBs; 1080p = 8160 MBs — mode decision cost scales linearly.
  • Reconstruction loop is non-optional: cannot skip even for speed; it is the correctness invariant.
  • Profile target: encode QCIF I-frame < 10 ms single-threaded at QP=28 (no SIMD yet).

6.8 Edge Cases

Edge case Handling
Non-multiple-of-16 frame dimensions pad to next MB boundary; PSNR computed only over valid region
Single-macroblock frame no left/top neighbors for MB(0,0); all DC→128
All-flat frame (uniform color) all modes produce identical prediction; DC chosen; residual all-zero; CBP=0
QP=0 encode residual levels large; placeholder exp-Golomb coding must handle ±32767
Width = 0 or height = 0 return Err(InvalidDimensions) before allocation
Corrupt bitstream length field BitReader returns Err(UnexpectedEof) on first out-of-bounds read
Reconstruction produces value > 255 clip to 255; no panic; test with QP=0 + high-frequency content

6.9 Failure Analysis

Failure Detection Blast radius Recovery
Encoder/decoder mismatch (I2 violated) encoder_recon XOR decoder_output nonzero all frames after first mismatch wrong add per-MB pixel-diff log; bisect to first failing MB
PSNR below floor automated CI check fails indicates wrong quant or transform compare individual crate unit tests; isolate quant vs transform
Panic on malformed bitstream fuzz input triggers panic process crash add catch_unwind wrapper in CLI; log NAL offset
Infinite loop in mode-decision encode hangs single frame stuck add loop-count assertion; ensure SAD terminates
Output file truncated partial bitstream written downstream decode fails use buffered write + explicit flush() + file-size check

6.10 Operational Notes

  • P10 metrics emitted per frame: {frame_poc, frame_type=I, qp, bits, y_psnr, u_psnr, v_psnr, mode_dist} as structured log (JSON line or CSV).
  • vc-cli inspect subcommand (stub, wired in Phase 14) should parse and print slice/MB headers — bitstream self-description planned from Phase 2.
  • No DPB state in Phase 6 (I-only); DPB introduced Phase 10.
  • Reconstruction frame buffer: allocated once per frame, reused across MBs; freed after output write.
  • Phase 7 will replace placeholder entropy with CAVLC; vc-encoder and vc-decoder call an EntropyEncoder/EntropyDecoder trait — stub that trait here so Phase 7 only swaps the implementation.
  • CI: add test_intra_roundtrip integration test that encodes + decodes foreman_qcif_10f.yuv and asserts byte-exact reconstruction + PSNR bounds.

Phase 7 — CAVLC Entropy Coding

7.1 Objectives

  • Replace placeholder coeff coding from Phase 6 with full H.264 CAVLC in vc-entropy.
  • Encode/decode residual coefficients: coeff_token, trailing-ones signs, levels, total_zeros, run_before using the normative VLC tables.
  • Encode macroblock-type (mb_type), intra prediction modes, and motion vectors via exp-Golomb / mapped VLC.
  • Guarantee P7: decode(encode(symbols)) == symbols for all coefficient sequences at all QP values.
  • Produce well-formed RBSP embedded in NAL units parseable by vc-bitstream.

7.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

Module vc-entropy/src/cavlc.rs

Component Detail
VLC tables Static [[(u8,u8); N]; 3] tables for coeff_token (indexed by nC), total_zeros, run_before; embed from H.264 Annex B
nC context Derive from left/above MB non-zero count average; edge MBs use single neighbor or 0; lookup into coeff_token table
Encode path cavlc_encode_block(coeffs: &[i32;16], nC: i8, bw: &mut BitWriter) — scan in zigzag order, count trailing ones (magnitude 1, alternating sign), encode levels as signed magnitudes with first-level suffix-length adaptation, encode total_zeros, run_before in reverse scan order
Decode path cavlc_decode_block(br: &mut BitReader, nC: i8) -> [i32;16] — parse coeff_token, trailing-one signs, level prefix/suffix with suffix-length tracking, total_zeros, run_before; reconstruct zigzag positions, inverse-zigzag
Zigzag scan ZIGZAG_4x4: [u8;16] static; ZIGZAG_8x8: [u8;64]; used by both encode and decode
MB syntax encode_mb_header(mb: &Macroblock, bw: &mut BitWriter) — mb_type via ue(), IntraMode via rem_intra4x4_pred_mode / prev_intra_mode_flag, CBP via mapped VLC, mb_qp_delta via se()
MV coding encode_mvd(mvd: MotionVector, bw: &mut BitWriter) — mvd_l0/l1 x/y as se(); decode symmetric
CAVLC 2x2 DC chroma Separate coeff_token table (nC == -1); encode/decode 4 chroma DC coeffs
Suffix-length table suffix_length starts at 0 for first level; increments at defined magnitude thresholds per spec Table 9-6

vc-entropy/src/lib.rs: expose CavlcEncoder, CavlcDecoder structs wrapping BitWriter/BitReader respectively; implement EntropyEncoder / EntropyDecoder traits.

Integration with vc-encoder/vc-decoder: wire CavlcEncoder after vc-quant output; wire CavlcDecoder before vc-quant dequant in decoder.

vc-entropy/src/exp_golomb.rs: already exists from Phase 2 (vc-bitstream); re-export or depend on vc-bitstream for ue/se to avoid duplication. Anchors: P7 (entropy coding is losslessly invertible), I6 (entropy roundtrip invariant), ADR-12 (CAVLC before CABAC), Sayood (Required Reading Books table, entropy coding fundamentals).

7.3 Deliverable

  • vc-entropy crate compiles with CavlcEncoder and CavlcDecoder.
  • Phase 6 I-frame codec updated: CAVLC replaces placeholder coeff coding; bitstreams parseable by decoder.
  • CLI vc-cli inspect prints per-MB coeff counts, nC values, and entropy bits.
  • Round-trip property test passes over 10 000 random coefficient arrays.

7.4 Acceptance Criteria

Check Target
cavlc_roundtrip property test 10 000 random [i32;16] blocks, all QP, decode(encode(x)) == x
Intra I-frame encode/decode PSNR >= 38 dB at QP 28 on 256×144 luma plane (Akiyo or equivalent)
Bitstream size regression Within ±2% of Phase 6 placeholder on same content (entropy is tighter, confirm improvement)
nC context Verified by comparing MB-level bit counts against JM reference on same frame
mb_type / mvd roundtrip 1000 random MbType + MotionVector pairs roundtrip through se/ue
Reject malformed stream Fuzz: 100 random-bit RBSP blocks → cavlc_decode_block returns Err, no panic

7.6 Pitfalls

Pitfall Manifestation Root Cause Debug Signal
Wrong suffix_length increment threshold Level magnitudes decoded incorrectly after first large coefficient Misread Table 9-6; threshold is per spec not intuitive Diff decoded levels against expected at second non-zero coeff
nC off-by-one at left/top edge Wrong coeff_token table row; corrupt decode Edge MB uses non-existent neighbor; must clamp to 0 Log nC per MB; compare against JM trace
Zigzag scan index mismatch Coefficients placed in wrong position; PSNR cliff Using 8×8 table for 4×4 block or vice versa Unit-test: verify each position index individually
total_zeros table OOB when TotalCoeff==0 Panic or garbage No total_zeros coded when TotalCoeff==0 per spec; encoder must not write it Guard in encoder; property test with all-zero inputs
Trailing-ones sign bit ordering Sign flipped on last trailing one Sign bits packed MSB-first for trailing-one highest-frequency first Golden-vector test with known sign pattern
mb_qp_delta cumulative drift Decoded QP diverges from encoder after several MBs mb_qp_delta is delta from previous MB QP; decoder must accumulate correctly Log QP per MB; compare encoder and decoder
Chroma DC path not invoked Chroma residual uncoded cavlc_encode_block called with nC == -1 guard missing; luma path taken Check cbp chroma flag before call

7.7 Performance Concerns

  • VLC table lookup: &[[(u8,u8); N]] is hot path; ensure it fits in L1 data cache (~4 KB for all coeff_token rows).
  • BitWriter/BitReader word-level buffering: flush only on byte boundary; avoid per-bit write_u8 calls.
  • Zigzag table: ZIGZAG_4x4 as [u8;16] const; compiler constant-folds indexing.
  • nC context computation: access adjacent MB state from a compact NonZeroCount array ([i8; mb_count]) passed through encoder state; no heap allocation per MB.
  • Avoid per-block Vec allocation; pass &mut [i32;16] on stack.

7.8 Edge Cases

Case Handling
All-zero 4×4 block TotalCoeff == 0; coeff_token codes 0 TotalCoeff, 0 trailing ones; no level/run fields written
TotalCoeff == 16 total_zeros uses specific table; run_before for last coeff always 0 per spec
Max level magnitude (±2047 or higher at low QP) Suffix length must accommodate; use escape coding per spec if level exceeds VLC range
Frame boundary MB: no left neighbor nC from above only; if no above, nC = 0
Slice boundary mid-frame Context reset at slice boundary; nC does not cross slice
mb_qp_delta == 0 for all MBs Encoded as ue(0); decoder accumulates no change
B-frame MB type in later phases mb_type offset differs per slice type; table selection must use slice_type

7.9 Failure Analysis

Failure Detection Blast Radius Recovery
CAVLC desync after one corrupt block All subsequent MBs in slice garbage; PSNR collapses Entire slice unusable Slice-level resync (future phase); log error, conceal with last good frame
Table embed error (one wrong entry) One specific coeff pattern always wrong; hard to detect Rare but silent Golden-vector test with 200 hand-verified entries catches pre-release
nC context bug Wrong table, wrong TotalCoeff, cascading desync Frame-wide artifacts Compare per-MB nC log vs JM reference trace
Panic on malformed input OOB table index or slice underflow Process crash Fuzz-test gate; all table accesses bounds-checked

7.10 Operational Notes

  • RUST_LOG=vc_entropy=debug emits per-MB: nC, TotalCoeff, bits-for-block, mb_qp_delta.
  • vc-cli inspect <bitstream> prints CAVLC stats per slice: avg bits/MB, coeff histogram, CBP distribution.
  • Metric: total entropy bits per frame; tracked alongside PSNR in bench output.
  • No deployment/migration concerns in this phase: output bitstream format established; Phase 13 switches CABAC at flag.

Phase 8 — Motion Estimation

8.1 Objectives

  • Implement integer-pel block-matching motion estimation in vc-motion.
  • Support SAD and SATD cost metrics; full-search and fast-pattern (diamond/EPZS) search with predicted-MV initialization.
  • Support partition shapes: 16×16, 16×8, 8×16, 8×8 (sub-partitions deferred to integration in Phase 10).
  • Compute lambda-weighted RD cost: J = distortion + lambda * bits(MV).
  • Output per-block best MotionVector and reference index from RefList; used by inter encoder in Phase 10.

8.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

Module vc-motion/src/search.rs — MotionSearchConfig fields: search_range: i16 (e.g. ±16 pel), use_satd: bool, use_epzs: bool, lambda: f32, partitions: PartitionSet (bitmask of enabled partition shapes).

Function Contract
full_search(src, ref_plane, mv_pred, cfg) -> (MotionVector, u32) Exhaustive search in [-sr, +sr]²; return MV with minimum J
diamond_search(src, ref_plane, mv_pred, cfg) -> (MotionVector, u32) Large diamond (±2) until no improvement; then small diamond (±1); start from mv_pred
epzs_search(src, ref_plane, spatial_mvs, temporal_mv, cfg) -> (MotionVector, u32) EPZS predictor set: median-of-neighbors, above, above-right, left; check each; then diamond refinement
block_sad(src: &[u8], ref_blk: &[u8], w: usize, h: usize) -> u32 Sum of absolute differences; 4×4, 8×8, 8×16, 16×8, 16×16
block_satd(src: &[u8], ref_blk: &[u8], w: usize, h: usize) -> u32 4×4 Hadamard-transformed SAD (SATD); better rate-distortion correlation
mv_bits(mvd: MotionVector) -> u32 Estimated bits for MVD via se() length formula: 2*floor(log2(abs(v)+1))+1
rd_cost(distortion: u32, mv_bits: u32, lambda: f32) -> f32 distortion as f32 + lambda * mv_bits as f32

Module vc-motion/src/predictor.rs

Function Contract
median_mv(left, above, above_right) -> MotionVector Per H.264 spec: component-wise median of three MVs
build_predictor_set(mb_idx, mb_mv_map) -> Vec<MotionVector> Spatial predictors: left, above, above-right, above-left; temporal from co-located

Reference block fetch: ref_block(plane: &Plane, mv: MotionVector, x0: i32, y0: i32, w: usize, h: usize) -> &[u8]; clamp MV to picture boundary + search range; no sub-pixel here (Phase 9).

Partition loop in motion_estimate_mb: for every enabled partition shape in cfg.partitions, and for every partition position within the macroblock, search(partition_src, ref_plane, predictor, cfg) returns (mv, cost); the candidate replaces the stored entry in best_partitions whenever its cost is lower than the current best for that position.

vc-motion/src/lib.rs: expose MotionEstimator struct holding MotionSearchConfig; method estimate_mb(mb_pos, src_frame, ref_frame, prev_mvs) -> Vec<(Partition, MotionVector, u32)>.

Cost table: precompute MV_BITS_TABLE: [u8; 256] for MVD in range −127..127 to avoid per-call log2. Anchors: ADR-13 (motion-search algorithm choice), Tourapis (Required Reading Papers table, fast motion estimation).

8.3 Deliverable

  • vc-motion crate builds with full-search, diamond, and EPZS modes selectable at runtime.
  • Standalone bench: encode a 352×288 CIF sequence, log per-frame average SAD, search time, and MV distribution.
  • Unit test: full-search on synthetic block with known displacement finds exact MV.
  • Motion vector heat-map output via vc-cli inspect --mv-map.

8.4 Acceptance Criteria

Check Target
Full-search correctness Exact-match test: synthetic translated block, full-search finds MV within ±0 at zero noise
Diamond convergence Diamond result matches full-search within 5% cost on foreman_cif 10 frames
SATD vs SAD SATD produces measurably lower residual energy after transform+quant (property test)
Search speed Diamond search < 0.5 ms per 16×16 MB at sr=16 on single core (benchmark crate)
EPZS predictor hit rate >= 60% of MBs accept a predictor-set candidate on natural video
Lambda weighting Increasing lambda → smaller MVs (shorter search range effective); property validated
Memory No heap allocation per MB in hot search path; verified with cargo bench --features alloc-tracking

8.5 Conformance Fixtures

Fixture Purpose Expected Output
fixtures/motion/block_sad_vectors.bin 50 SAD test cases (src, ref, w, h, expected_sad) Exact match
fixtures/motion/block_satd_vectors.bin 30 SATD cases with Hadamard-computed reference Exact match
fixtures/motion/median_mv_cases.json 100 (left, above, above_right) → expected_median cases Exact
fixtures/motion/fullsearch_synthetic.yuv 16×16 block displaced 3,5 pel in known pattern MV == {12, 20} (qpel units) or {3,5} integer
fixtures/motion/epzs_predictor_trace.json Known predictor set → expected candidate ranking on 5 frames Match within tolerance
fixtures/motion/mv_bits_table.bin Expected bit lengths for MVD −63..63 Exact match against mv_bits()

8.6 Pitfalls

Pitfall Manifestation Root Cause Debug Signal
Reference block boundary not clamped SIGSEGV or garbage SAD for MBs near edge Negative MV + block offset reads outside plane allocation Add bounds check in ref_block; assert in debug build
Lambda not scaled to SAD units Lambda too large → all MBs coded as skip; too small → MV bits dominate Lambda must be in same scale as SADs (pixel^2 or abs units); typical lambda = 0.85 * 2^((QP-12)/3) Plot cost breakdown (dist vs lambda*bits) per frame
Diamond stuck in local minimum MV off by 1-2 pels from full-search; visible artifacts Early termination too aggressive; small-diamond not run after large Compare diamond vs full-search on challenging frame
SATD accumulates integer overflow Overflow at high-contrast 16×16 block Hadamard intermediate can reach 255*16 = 4080 per row; sum of 16 rows = 65280; fits u16 but intermediate products need i32 Use i32 accumulator throughout Hadamard
Predictor MV out of bounds EPZS starts search outside range; no valid candidate found Spatial MV from neighbor may already be at boundary; clamp predictor before search Clamp predictor set candidates to [-sr,+sr] before evaluation
Partition shape cost not comparable Wrong partition chosen (8×16 vs 16×16) Bits estimate for partition mode not added to J Add mode_bits(partition_shape) term via ue() length estimate
median_mv component-wise wrong Wrong MV prediction; high MVD bits coded Median applied globally not per-component Unit test each component of median independently

8.7 Performance Concerns

  • SAD inner loop: process 16 bytes/row with SIMD-friendly layout; Plane.stride must be >= width; ensure 16-byte alignment.
  • ref_block pointer arithmetic: compute once per MV candidate, not per pixel; pass as slice to SAD function.
  • Loop order: iterate over reference MV candidates in outer loop, pixel accumulation in inner; maximizes register reuse.
  • Diamond pattern array: const LARGE_DIAMOND: [(i16,i16); 8], const SMALL_DIAMOND: [(i16,i16); 4] — branch-free iteration.
  • Cache: for sr=16, full-search checks 33×33 = 1089 positions; keep reference plane slice in L2 (352×288 Y plane = ~100 KB, fits L2).
  • Avoid f32 in hot path: precompute lambda_int = (lambda * 256.0) as u32; cost = sad + (lambda_int * mv_bits) >> 8.

8.8 Edge Cases

Case Handling
MV at frame boundary (top-left MB, all neighbors unavailable) Predictor = (0,0); full-search from origin
Zero-motion scene All MVs (0,0); skip MB detection in Phase 10 based on this
search_range == 0 Only zero-motion checked; treated as all-skip encoder
Single-row or single-column video Partition shapes reduced to 16×8 or 8×16 only; 8×8 partitions clamped
Identical source and reference (lossless reconstruction test) SAD == 0 at true MV; full-search returns correct MV with cost = lambda * bits
All-equal reference plane (flat scene) All MVs tied at 0 cost; return (0,0) by convention
ref_list empty P-frame with no reference; must not call motion estimator; guard in vc-encoder

8.9 Failure Analysis

Failure Detection Blast Radius Recovery
Wrong SAD returns incorrect MV All inter MBs have high residual; PSNR 5+ dB below expected Entire P-frame quality degraded Unit test SAD with synthetic displaced blocks catches pre-release
Search range OOB panic Process abort on near-edge MB Single frame encode crash Bounds-check in ref_block; fuzz with tiny frame dimensions
Lambda = 0.0 (config bug) Encoder ignores MV bits; picks large MVs; bloated bitstream Bitrate overflow Assert lambda > 0.0 in MotionSearchConfig constructor
EPZS predictor references freed DPB frame Stale pointer dereference Undefined behavior vc-motion borrows &Plane with explicit lifetime; borrow checker enforces

8.10 Operational Notes

  • RUST_LOG=vc_motion=debug: per-MB log of chosen search mode, MV, cost, partition shape.
  • vc-cli inspect --mv-map <bitstream> renders ASCII MV field (arrow glyphs per MB row).
  • Per-frame metrics logged to structured JSON: {frame, avg_sad, avg_mv_magnitude, search_mode, total_search_ms}.
  • Rate control in Phase 13 consumes per-frame avg SAD as scene-complexity signal; log it here.
  • No migration concern: vc-motion is a pure compute crate; API changes are internal until Phase 10 integration.

Phase 9 — Sub-Pixel Motion Compensation

9.1 Objectives

  • Implement half-pel and quarter-pel interpolation in vc-inter for luma (1/8-pel chroma): half-pel luma uses the 6-tap filter [1,-5,20,20,-5,1]/32 specified in H.264 sec. 8.4.2; quarter-pel luma is bilinear averaging between adjacent half-pel/integer-pel positions; quarter-pel chroma is 1/8-pel bilinear interpolation.
  • Fetch motion-compensated prediction block from Plane with correct edge extension, and supply the predictor block for inter MB residual computation in Phase 10.

9.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

Module vc-inter/src/interpolate.rs

Luma sub-pixel position naming per H.264 spec (letters a–s for half-/quarter-pel positions):

Integer positions:  G  b  H
                    d  e  f
                    A  h  B
                    (H.264 Fig 8-2 layout, abcdefghijklmnopqrs positions)

Half-pel position b is the 6-tap filter [G,d,h,n,s,M] applied horizontally; position h is the same 6-tap filter applied vertically over [A,G,B,C,D,E]; the diagonal position j applies the 6-tap filter twice (the vertical filter over an intermediate horizontally-filtered column). Each quarter-pel position is the bilinear average of its two adjacent integer/half-pel neighbors — e.g. a = avg(G, b), e = avg(b, h), i = avg(h, j) — for every remaining quarter-pel letter in the Fig 8-2 layout.

Function Contract
luma_half_pel_h(src: &Plane, x: i32, y: i32, w: usize, h: usize, dst: &mut [u8]) Horizontal 6-tap at half-pel; clamp to [0,255]; edge-extend by replication
luma_half_pel_v(src: &Plane, x: i32, y: i32, w: usize, h: usize, dst: &mut [u8]) Vertical 6-tap
luma_half_pel_d(src: &Plane, x: i32, y: i32, w: usize, h: usize, dst: &mut [u8]) Diagonal (h+v 6-tap); intermediate values kept as i32 before final shift
luma_quarter_pel(h0: &[u8], h1: &[u8], w: usize, h: usize, dst: &mut [u8]) (a+b+1)>>1 bilinear between two adjacent half-/integer-pel blocks
chroma_interp(src: &Plane, mv: MotionVector, x0: i32, y0: i32, w: usize, h: usize, dst: &mut [u8]) Chroma 1/8-pel: (1-dx)(1-dy)A + dx(1-dy)B + (1-dx)dy*C + dx*dy*D; dx,dy in 1/8 units

Edge extension (vc-inter/src/edge_extend.rs):

Method Rule
Replication padding Column/row 0 replicated left; column W-1 replicated right; analogous for top/bottom
padded_sample(plane, x, y) -> u8 Clamp x to [0, width-1], y to [0, height-1]; read plane.data[y*stride + x]
6-tap lookahead Requires 2 samples beyond block boundary in each direction; boundary check per tap

MC fetch (vc-inter/src/mc.rs): fetch_luma_block(ref_plane: &Plane, mv: MotionVector, mb_x: i32, mb_y: i32, w: usize, h: usize, dst: &mut [u8]) dispatches on (mv.x & 3, mv.y & 3): integer position copies directly, half-pel applies the 6-tap filter, quarter-pel applies bilinear averaging.

Quarter-pel position selection table (luma):

qpel_x \ qpel_y 0 1 2 3
0 int-copy v-half v-half+1-avg v-qpel
1 h-half diag avg(h+diag) avg(v+diag)
2 h-half+1-avg avg(h+diag) diag avg(v+diag)
3 h-qpel avg(h+diag) avg(v+diag) diag

vc-inter/src/lib.rs: expose MotionCompensator struct; method compensate(ref_frame: &Frame, mv: MotionVector, mb_x, mb_y, part_w, part_h, dst_luma: &mut [u8], dst_cb: &mut [u8], dst_cr: &mut [u8]).

Bi-prediction (Phase 11 prep): stub bipredict(pred0: &[u8], pred1: &[u8], dst: &mut [u8]) → (a+b+1)>>1; wired fully in Phase 11. Anchors: P4 (causal prediction — valid DPB entries only), ADR-14 (6-tap half-pel filter choice), Girod (Required Reading Papers table, sub-pixel interpolation), H.264 sec. 8.4.2 (luma sample interpolation).

9.3 Deliverable

  • vc-inter crate builds with MotionCompensator::compensate.
  • Unit tests verify exact half-pel filter output against known reference values.
  • Sub-pel interpolation available to Phase 10 inter encoder; integer-pel path also available as fallback.
  • Bench: interpolate 1000 16×16 luma blocks at all 4 quarter-pel positions; measure throughput (blocks/sec).

9.4 Acceptance Criteria

Check Target
Half-pel luma filter exact match 10 hand-computed 4×4 cases from H.264 spec match luma_half_pel_h/v/d output exactly
Chroma 1/8-pel bilinear exact match 20 cases with known dx,dy match chroma_interp output exactly

Throughput >= 500 MB/s luma interpolation throughput for 16×16 blocks on single core

Fixture Purpose Expected Output
fixtures/inter/half_pel_h_vectors.bin, half_pel_v_vectors.bin, half_pel_d_vectors.bin 10 luma rows/columns/diagonal cases + filter params + expected output each Exact byte match
fixtures/inter/qpel_all16_cases.bin 16 qpel positions × 5 blocks each Exact match per position

9.6 Pitfalls

Pitfall Manifestation Root Cause Debug Signal
Intermediate 6-tap overflow Artifacts (clipping artifacts) at high-contrast edges [1,-5,20,20,-5,1] applied to max-value row: 20255 + 20255 = 10200 > i16::MAX Use i32 for intermediate accumulation; shift and clamp at end
Wrong filter shift (divide by 32 vs 64) Brightness offset in reconstructed blocks Spec: shift by 5 (divide by 32) not 6; common off-by-one Unit test: flat all-128 block should produce 128 at all sub-pel positions
Quarter-pel bilinear rounding One-pixel off in specific quarter-pel positions Rounding: (a+b+1)>>1 not (a+b)>>1; spec mandates round-half-up Golden-vector test with value 127, 128 combinations
Chroma MV scaling Chroma block shifted by wrong amount Chroma MV = luma MV / 2 (for 4:2:0); 1/8-pel chroma from half-pel luma MV Verify chroma MC on checkerboard with known luma MV
6-tap tap out of reachable plane Read 2 samples beyond block in each direction; no padding buffer Edge extension not applied before filter; direct plane access at boundary assert in padded_sample; run with -fsanitize=address equivalent (Miri)
B-frame wrong predictor averaging; stride-based access in wrong dimension Blocking artifacts at P/B mix boundary; column filter reads row data Bi-prediction stub returns pred0 only; vertical filter must step by stride not 1 Confirm avg wiring in Phase 11; unit test non-square stride, check column output

9.7 Performance Concerns

  • 6-tap horizontal: process width bytes per row; inner loop: 6 multiply-adds per output sample; SIMD target is 8–16 output samples per cycle. Intermediate buffer for diagonal 6-tap: allocate a [(h+5) * w] temp buffer on stack (max 21 × 16 = 336 bytes for 16×16 block); avoid heap.
  • Edge extension: compute padded index via min/max clamp — branch-free on most architectures. compensate hot path dispatches on (mv.x & 3, mv.y & 3) with a 4×4 function-pointer table to eliminate branch chains.
  • Chroma blocks are 8×8 for 16×16 MB (4:2:0 half-size); interpolation cost is 1/4 of luma — optimize luma first, and avoid re-interpolating the same sub-pel position twice for B-frame bi-prediction (cache the two prediction blocks).

9.8 Edge Cases

Case Handling
MV points entirely outside frame After edge-extension clamping, entire block is replicated boundary; valid though inefficient
MV == (0, 0) in quarter-pel units Integer-copy path; no filter computation; fast path
part_w or part_h not multiple of 4 Not valid in H.264 partitions; assert in debug builds
Plane stride > width; chroma plane width/height odd Filter must use stride not width for row advancement; 4:2:0 requires even luma dimensions, asserted at frame creation in vc-color
B-frame ref list 1 empty compensate called with null ref1; bi-prediction stub must guard; Phase 11 fills fully
QP change mid-frame (future rate control) MC is QP-independent; no action

9.9 Failure Analysis

Failure Detection Blast Radius Recovery
Wrong half-pel filter All sub-pel inter MBs have excess residual; PSNR 3-8 dB below expected Entire P/B frame quality Golden-vector unit tests with spec-computed values catch before integration
OOB read in edge extension Miri error or SIGSEGV Single frame decode crash Miri CI gate; bounds-check in padded_sample
Chroma MV scale error; rounding error accumulation in 6-tap Chroma channel blocky, luma clean; slight DC offset over multiple reference frames Silent quality bug; gradual drift (caught by I2) Compute chroma PSNR separately, alert if > 3 dB gap vs luma; compare encoder vs decoder output pixel-by-pixel per frame

9.10 Operational Notes

  • RUST_LOG=vc_inter=trace: per-block log of MV, sub-pel position, interpolation mode selected. Bench output: sub_pel_throughput_luma_16x16: <N> Mpixels/s and sub_pel_throughput_chroma_8x8: <N> Mpixels/s.
  • Per-frame metric: fraction of MBs using integer vs half vs quarter-pel MV (logged as mv_precision_histogram).
  • Phase 10 integration: MotionCompensator::compensate called after MotionEstimator::estimate_mb refines to quarter-pel; the two crates are composed in vc-encoder's per-MB loop. Phase 11 promotes the bipredict stub to full weighted averaging with ref_list1 DPB lookup.
  • No external deployment concerns; vc-inter is a pure compute crate with no I/O.

Phase 10 — Inter Prediction (P-frames)

10.1 Objectives

  • Implement complete P-slice encode/decode path in vc-inter and vc-encoder/vc-decoder.
  • MV prediction: median-of-3-neighbors (MVDP per H.264 spec), MVD coding via exp-Golomb.
  • Skip macroblock detection: zero MVD + zero CBP → coded as Skip, no residual bits.
  • Integrate motion search (Phase 8) → MC (Phase 9) → residual → transform/quant/CAVLC → reconstruction loop.
  • Single reference frame (ref_idx=0, list0 only) for initial P-slice; multi-ref deferred to Phase 11.
  • Constraint: encoder reconstructed frames used as DPB references (P2 — drift-free encoder reconstruction loop).

10.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

vc-inter additions (src/inter/p_slice.rs):

  • fn mv_predict(mb_pos: MbPos, dpb: &Dpb, refs: &RefList) -> MotionVector — median of A (left), B (above), C (above-right) neighbors; if C unavailable use D (above-left); edge cases: left edge → A=0, top edge → B=0.
  • fn encode_skip(mb: &Macroblock) -> bool — check MVD==0 AND CBP==0 AND ref_idx==0; emit mb_skip_run increment, no further syntax.
  • fn residual_inter(src: &Plane, pred: &Plane, mb_pos: MbPos) -> [TransformBlock; 16] — src - pred per 4x4 luma partition; chroma Cb/Cr 8x8 split into four 4x4 blocks each.
  • CBP (Coded Block Pattern): bitmask of 4 luma 8x8 regions + chroma; set bit iff any nonzero coeff in that region after quantization.

vc-encoder P-slice loop (src/encoder/p_slice.rs):

  • Per macroblock: run intra RD (Phase 5+7) AND inter RD (motion search + MC → residual → transform → quant → entropy bit estimate); select mode with minimum lambda * bits + SSD.
  • Skip check before mode decision: if co-located MV from previous frame is near-zero, probe skip cost first.
  • After mode selection: inverse-quant → inverse-DCT → add prediction → clamp → store in reconstruction buffer.
  • Pass reconstructed luma/chroma to vc-deblock boundary accumulators (populated in Phase 12; stub call now).
  • After full slice reconstruction: push frame into Dpb, mark as short-term reference.

vc-decoder P-slice loop (src/decoder/p_slice.rs):

  • Parse mb_skip_run; for skip MBs: derive MV = MVP, no residual, reconstruct = MC block, feed into DPB.
  • For coded MBs: parse mb_type, ref_idx, MVD, CBP, residual (CAVLC); dequant; inverse-DCT; add MC prediction; clamp.
  • SliceHeader fields used: slice_type=P, frame_num, num_ref_idx_l0_active_minus1, qp_delta.

vc-bitstream additions:

  • write_mvd(bw: &mut BitWriter, mvd: MotionVector) — se(v) for mvd_l0[x] and mvd_l0[y].
  • write_cbp(bw: &mut BitWriter, cbp: u8, mb_type: MbType) — mapped VLC (intra) or ue(v) (inter) per spec Table 9-36.
  • parse_mb_skip_run(br: &mut BitReader) -> u32 — ue(v).

Testing harness (tests/p_slice.rs):

  • Encode 10-frame sequence (1 IDR + 9 P); decode; compare PSNR per frame.
  • Skip-only sequence: all-flat input → encoder must emit all skips; verify bitstream length near-minimal.
  • Unit test mv_predict with known neighbor MVs against spec examples (Table 8-4 region). Anchors: P2 (drift-free reconstruction loop, extended from I-frames to P-frames in this phase), I2 (encoder/decoder DPB-match invariant), ADR-13/ADR-14 (motion search + interpolation reused unchanged for P-slices).

10.3 Deliverable

  • vc_encoder::encode_p_slice + vc_decoder::decode_p_slice compile and pass integration tests.
  • CLI: vc-cli encode --input seq.yuv --frames 10 --gop I1P9 --output out.264 produces parseable bitstream.
  • vc-cli psnr --ref seq.yuv --rec out_dec.yuv prints per-frame Y/Cb/Cr PSNR.
  • Skip macroblock ratio visible in encoder log (P10 observability).

10.4 Acceptance Criteria

Criterion Target
PSNR Y (CIF, QP=28, 10P frames) ≥ 30 dB
Skip MB ratio (low-motion input) ≥ 40 %
mv_predict unit tests 100 % pass
Roundtrip: decode(encode(seq)) bit-exact reconstruction I2 satisfied
Encoder/decoder frame count match exact
Fuzz: 1000 random MVD values parse(write(v))==v 100 %

10.5 Conformance Fixtures

Fixture Purpose Expected Output
p_flat_cif.yuv flat input → all-skip bitstream < 200 B per P-frame
p_motion_qcif.yuv moderate motion PSNR Y ≥ 30 dB @ QP 28
p_edge_topleft.yuv MB at (0,0): no A/B/C neighbors MVP = (0,0)
p_mvd_extremes.bin MVD=±127, ±255 parse OK; se(v) roundtrip
p_cbp_patterns.bin all 48 CBP values (inter) ue(v) roundtrip
p_skip_run_max.bin skip_run=255 ue(v) parse OK

10.6 Pitfalls

  • MVP boundary off-by-one: when MB is at left edge, neighbor A is unavailable → use (0,0), not garbage memory. Root cause: forgetting edge-availability flag. Debug: log MVP per MB; compare against JM reference.
  • CBP luma-vs-chroma bit layout: H.264 inter CBP is ue(v) with a specific 2-MSB chroma field; intra CBP is mapped VLC. Mixing tables → wrong CBP → missing residual. Signal: nonzero coeffs decoded as zero.
  • Drift from original-based prediction: if encoder predicts from original rather than reconstructed frame (violates P2), PSNR degrades sharply after 3–4 P-frames. Debug: subtract encoder recon from decoder recon; should be zero.
  • Skip-run count overflow: skip_run is ue(v); must be flushed before each coded MB. Missing flush → wrong MB addresses. Debug: count skips vs coded MBs in bitstream inspector.
  • MVD sign convention: se(v) maps even codes to positive, odd to negative; off-by-one in sign mapping causes systematic half-pel drift. Verify with known MV pairs.
  • Chroma MV scaling: chroma MVs are luma MVs / 2 (4:2:0); forgetting the shift causes wrong chroma MC. Root cause: copy-paste from luma path.

10.7 Performance Concerns

  • Motion search (Phase 8) dominates P-slice encode time; SATD in inner loop — keep 4x4 SATD branch-free.
  • MC fetch for each partition: stride-aware reads from DPB reference plane; cache miss on large reference frames — prefetch 16 bytes ahead in quarter-pel interpolation inner loop.
  • Per-MB mode decision runs intra + inter cost; cache intra residual if not selected (avoid re-computing).
  • Skip detection short-circuits the full MB pipeline — check skip first to avoid motion search on static regions.
  • Vec<Partition> allocation per MB: use a fixed-size array [Partition; 16] with a count field to avoid heap allocation in the hot loop.

10.8 Edge Cases

  • First P-frame: DPB has exactly one reference (the IDR); ref_idx=0 always valid.
  • MV pointing outside frame bounds: clip to padded reference border (replicate padding, not wrap).
  • QP=0: quantization step=1; all coefficients nonzero; CBP = 0x3F; test that entropy coder handles max coefficient magnitudes.
  • QP=51: heavy quantization; nearly all coefficients zero → near-all-skip behavior even on high-motion input; PSNR expected low.
  • Single-row sequence (height=16): only one MB row; B and C neighbors always unavailable.
  • Integer overflow in SSD accumulator: 16-bit residual squared × 256 samples = up to 256 × 65535 ≈ 16M; use i32 accumulator.

10.9 Failure Analysis

Failure Detection Blast Radius Recovery
MVP bug PSNR drop after frame 1; MV histogram outliers All P-frames in GOP Fix neighbor availability; re-encode
Drift (P2 violated) Decoder diverges after ~5 frames; PSNR collapses Entire GOP after IDR Enforce reconstruction loop; re-encode
CBP parse mismatch Decoder reads wrong coeff count; bit desync Remainder of slice Add sync-point (re-use IDR)
Skip-run flush missing Macroblocks misaligned; garbled output Entire P-slice Flush run before every coded MB write

10.10 Operational Notes

  • Log per-frame: frame_num, slice_type, skip_mb_count, coded_mb_count, total_bits, Y-PSNR.
  • Encoder reconstruction error metric: max(abs(enc_recon - dec_recon)) per frame; assert == 0 in debug builds (I2).
  • vc-cli inspect subcommand: print MB-type map (I/P/Skip) per frame for visual verification.
  • Rate-control hook: record actual bits per frame for QP feedback in Phase 13.
  • DPB state dump: log reference frame poc values held in DPB after each frame; aids debugging reference management.

Phase 11 — B-frames + GOP + Reference Management

11.1 Objectives

  • Add B-slice bi-prediction: two reference lists (list0 = past, list1 = future), weighted-average prediction.
  • Implement GOP cadence: IDR(1) + P(N) + B(M) pattern; default IBBP and IBP variants configurable.
  • Picture Order Count (POC): type 0 (frame_num based) and type 2 (display order) per H.264 spec.
  • DPB reference list construction: RefPicListModification, short-term/long-term marking, sliding window.
  • Hierarchical-B (two-level) optional behind a compile flag.
  • Constraint: I4 (prediction causality) — B-frame predictor references only already-decoded frames in DPB.

11.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

vc-encoder GOP manager (src/encoder/gop.rs):

  • struct GopConfig { idr_period: u32, p_period: u32, b_count: u32, hierarchical: bool }.
  • fn gop_schedule(config: &GopConfig, display_idx: u32) -> FrameType — emit IDR, then repeat [B*b_count, P] pattern; output includes poc and decode_order vs display_order.
  • Reorder input queue: buffer b_count B-frames before the anchor P-frame; encode anchor P first (decode order), then B-frames.

Dpb management (vc-core/src/dpb.rs):

  • fn dpb_mark_reference(dpb: &mut Dpb, frame: Frame, marking: RefMarking) — short-term vs long-term; sliding window eviction when max_dpb_size reached.
  • fn build_ref_list0(dpb: &Dpb, curr_poc: i32) -> RefList — frames with poc < curr_poc, sorted by poc descending.
  • fn build_ref_list1(dpb: &Dpb, curr_poc: i32) -> RefList — frames with poc > curr_poc, sorted by poc ascending.
  • fn reorder_ref_list(list: &mut RefList, modifications: &[RefPicListMod]) — apply RefPicListModification entries (reorder by abs_diff_pic_num).
  • max_dpb_size derived from level and resolution; default 4 for SD resolution in this implementation.

B-slice encoder (src/encoder/b_slice.rs):

  • Per MB: run intra, inter-list0, inter-list1, bi-pred modes; bi-pred prediction = (pred0 + pred1 + 1) >> 1 (equal-weight).
  • fn bi_pred_block(ref0: &Plane, mv0: MotionVector, ref1: &Plane, mv1: MotionVector, dst: &mut Plane) — fetch MC from both references, average.
  • MVD coding: two sets of MVDs (list0 + list1) via write_mvd; ref_idx for each list via te(v) or ue(v) per num_ref.
  • Skip / Direct mode: derive MV from co-located P-frame MB (temporal direct) or spatial neighbors.
  • B-frame reconstruction: identical pipeline to P-frame; push to DPB only if marked as reference (anchor B in hierarchical mode).

vc-decoder B-slice loop (src/decoder/b_slice.rs):

  • Parse slice_type=B; construct list0 and list1 from DPB; parse MVDs for both lists; perform bi-pred MC; add residual; deblock stub.
  • POC derivation: TopFieldOrderCnt = 2 * (frame_num * MaxFrameNum + offset) (type 2 simplified).

vc-bitstream additions:

  • write_ref_idx(bw, ref_idx, num_active) — te(v) coded.
  • write_direct_mode_flag(bw, direct: bool).
  • Slice header: write direct_spatial_mv_pred_flag, num_ref_idx_l0/l1_active.

Testing (tests/b_gop.rs):

  • Encode 13-frame IBBPBBP sequence; decode in display order; compare PSNR with P-only baseline.
  • DPB eviction test: 20-frame sequence with max_dpb_size=4; verify no use-after-evict.
  • POC ordering test: decode-order index vs display-order POC mapping table. Anchors: P11 (GOP structure is declarative), I8 (POC ordering invariant), I12 (DPB occupancy / B-frame ref-list bound), ADR-28 (explicit DPB eviction via Vec::remove).

11.3 Deliverable

  • CLI: vc-cli encode --gop IBBP --frames 13 produces IBBP bitstream; decoder outputs 13 frames in display order.
  • vc-cli psnr shows B-frame PSNR competitive with adjacent P-frames at same QP.
  • DPB state log shows correct short-term reference list per slice.

11.4 Acceptance Criteria

Criterion Target
PSNR Y (CIF, QP=28, IBBP GOP) ≥ P-only baseline ± 0.5 dB
Display-order frame reconstruction exact (all 13 frames present, no duplicates)
DPB size never exceeds max_dpb_size assert in debug builds
I4 satisfied: no B-frame refs future undecoded frame static analysis + runtime check
POC roundtrip for 100-frame sequence zero ordering errors
build_ref_list0/1 unit tests 20 cases covering edge POCs

11.5 Conformance Fixtures

Fixture Purpose Expected Output
gop_ibbp_cif.yuv standard IBBP 13-frame PSNR Y ≥ 30 dB @ QP 28
gop_ibp_short.yuv 5-frame IBP 5 decoded frames, correct POC order
dpb_evict_test.264 forces DPB eviction at frame 5 frames 1-4 evicted; frame 5+ valid
direct_mode_flat.yuv all-direct-mode B-frames bitstream near-minimal; decode correct
poc_wrap.264 frame_num wraps at MaxFrameNum=256 POC continuity maintained
ref_list_mod.264 explicit RefPicListModification list rebuilt per modification entries

11.6 Pitfalls

  • Display/decode order confusion: B-frames are encoded before their display position; writing them to output in decode order corrupts the sequence. Root cause: missing reorder buffer. Debug: print (decode_idx, poc) pairs.
  • DPB use-after-evict: sliding-window eviction frees a frame still referenced by a B-frame's list1. Root cause: eviction happens before all B-frames using that reference are encoded. Debug: reference-count DPB entries; assert refcount > 0 before evict.
  • Temporal direct MV derivation: co-located MB must exist in the previously decoded P-frame; if co-located is a skip, use zero MV. Missing skip check → random MV read.
  • POC type mismatch: encoder and decoder compute POC by different formulas if pic_order_cnt_type field is inconsistent in SPS. Verify SPS is written and parsed before slice headers.
  • Bi-pred rounding: (a + b + 1) >> 1 (round half up); using (a + b) >> 1 introduces a half-LSB offset accumulated over many frames. Signal: subtle PSNR degradation on B-frames.
  • List1 construction for last-GOP B-frame: if the future anchor P is the last frame, list1 may be empty; fall back to list0-only prediction for those MBs.

11.7 Performance Concerns

  • B-frame reorder buffer: holds b_count raw input frames in memory; for HD, 2 B-frames × 1080p YUV = 2 × 3 MB; pre-allocate.
  • build_ref_list0/1: linear scan over DPB (≤ 4 frames) — negligible; avoid repeated rebuilds per MB.
  • Bi-pred MC: two MC fetches per block vs one for P; memory bandwidth doubles for inter MBs; batch reference frame reads.
  • DPB eviction: Vec::remove(idx) shifts elements; use a ring buffer or a fixed-size array with index wrap to avoid O(N) shift.
  • Mode decision with B-frames: intra + list0 + list1 + bi-pred = 4 paths per MB; cache SAD values; skip list1 if poc_delta large and SAD(list0) is very low.

11.8 Edge Cases

  • Only one reference in list1 (final P-frame of GOP): num_ref_idx_l1_active = 1; te(v) ref_idx = 0 always.
  • GOP shorter than b_count + 1: trailing B-frames have no future anchor; encoder must flush them as P-frames or as B-frames with list0-only.
  • IDR resets DPB: all references evicted; first frame after IDR has empty list0 and list1; must be an I-frame.
  • B-frame as reference (hierarchical-B): when b_count=2 hierarchical, the level-1 B at POC midpoint is a reference; mark it in DPB before encoding level-2 B-frames.
  • abs_diff_pic_num overflow in RefPicListModification: modular arithmetic mod MaxFrameNum; implement with wrapping subtraction.

11.9 Failure Analysis

Failure Detection Blast Radius Recovery
Display-order corruption First B-frame incorrect; obvious visual glitch All B-frames in GOP Fix reorder buffer; check POC sort
DPB use-after-evict Segfault or wrong pixels from freed frame Frames after eviction event Add refcount; evict only when refcount == 0
Empty list1 crash Panic on list1[0] access All B-frames Guard: fall back to list0 if list1 empty
POC desync Decoder output in wrong order Entire sequence after desync Re-read SPS poc_type field; assert match

11.10 Operational Notes

  • Log per-frame: poc, decode_order, frame_type, ref_list0_pocs, ref_list1_pocs, bits, Y-PSNR.
  • DPB dump command: vc-cli inspect --dpb-state out.264 lists frames in DPB at each decode point.
  • Metric: B-frame compression efficiency = B-frame bits / adjacent-P-frame bits; target < 0.7 for low-motion.
  • Hierarchical-B flag in encoder config YAML; disabled by default; enable via --hierarchical-b.
  • Reorder-buffer depth exposed as encoder.reorder_depth in metrics; aids latency analysis.

Phase 12 — In-Loop Deblocking Filter

12.1 Objectives

  • Implement vc-deblock: compute boundary strength (BS) per 4x4 edge; apply adaptive luma + chroma edge filter.
  • Filter applied inside reconstruction loop in both encoder and decoder identically (P6 — in-loop filtering is inside the loop).
  • Thresholds alpha, beta derived from QP; tc0 table for the filter strength.
  • Filter luma edges (horizontal + vertical) at 4x4 granularity; chroma at 8x8 granularity.
  • I9 (deblocking determinism): BS and filtered samples are a deterministic function of reconstructed data only.

12.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

vc-deblock (src/deblock/mod.rs):

Boundary strength computation (src/deblock/bs.rs):

BS=4: intra macroblock boundary (strongest)
BS=3: inter MB boundary with coded coefficients
BS=2: inter MB boundary, no coefficients, |mv_diff| >= 1 pel OR ref_idx diff
BS=1: no coeff, |mv_diff| < 1 pel, same ref
BS=0: no filtering
  • fn boundary_strength(mb_a: &Macroblock, mb_b: &Macroblock, edge: Edge) -> u8 — Edge encodes {axis: H|V, position: 0..3 in 4x4 units}.
  • Intra check: if either MB is intra → BS=4 (P-frame boundary) or BS=3/4 (per H.264 Table 8-18).
  • MV difference: |mv_a.x - mv_b.x| >= 4 (quarter-pel units = 1 integer pel) → contributes to BS=2.

Filter application (src/deblock/filter.rs):

  • fn filter_luma_edge(plane: &mut Plane, pos: PixelPos, axis: Axis, bs: u8, alpha: i32, beta: i32) — modifies p0/p1/p2/q0/q1/q2 in-place.
  • BS=4 strong filter: p2' = (p2+2*p1+2*p0+2*q0+q1+4)/8; p1'=(p2+p1+p0+q0+2)/4; p0'=(p2+2*p1+2*p0+2*q0+q1+4)/8 (symmetric).
  • BS=1..3 normal filter: clip delta = (((q0-p0)*4 + (p1-q1) + 4) >> 3) to [-tc0, tc0]; p0+=delta, q0-=delta; p1/q1 conditionally updated using tc0 table.
  • fn compute_thresholds(qp_avg: u8) -> (i32, i32) — alpha = alpha_table[clip3(0,51,qp_avg+alpha_offset)]; beta = beta_table[...+beta_offset]; tables from H.264 spec Table 8-16.
  • fn tc0_value(bs: u8, qp: u8) -> i32 — from spec Table 8-17.
  • Chroma filter: same BS table but QP is chroma QP (mapped from luma QP); filter only p0/q0 (no p1/q1 update); edges at 8x8 boundaries only.

Filter scheduling (src/deblock/scheduler.rs):

  • Process frame left-to-right, top-to-bottom; for each MB: filter left vertical edge first, then top horizontal edge; order ensures p-side samples already filtered when q-side is processed.
  • fn deblock_frame(frame: &mut Frame, mbs: &[Macroblock], qp_map: &[u8]) — outer loop over MBs; inner: 4 vertical edges × 4 positions, 4 horizontal edges × 4 positions.

Integration in reconstruction loop:

  • Encoder: after vc-transform inverse + prediction add → store in recon buffer → call deblock_frame → push to DPB.
  • Decoder: identical call site; same deblock_frame function (shared via vc-deblock crate).
  • Deblocking parameters disable_deblocking_filter_idc (0=on, 1=off, 2=slice-boundary-only) read from slice header.

Testing (tests/deblock.rs):

  • Synthesize two adjacent MBs with known content, known BS; compute filtered output; compare against hand-calculated reference.
  • Full-frame test: encode + decode with and without deblocking; verify PSNR-with-deblock > PSNR-without at QP≥28.
  • Determinism test: apply deblock_frame twice → second application changes no pixels (idempotent given fixed input). Anchors: P6 (in-loop filtering is inside the loop), I9 (deblock determinism invariant), List/Joch/Lainema/Bjøntegaard/Karczewicz (Required Reading Papers table, adaptive deblocking filter).

12.3 Deliverable

  • vc-deblock::deblock_frame compiles; integrated in encoder reconstruction loop and decoder output path.
  • CLI encodes with deblocking enabled by default; --no-deblock flag disables for comparison.
  • Per-frame PSNR with vs without deblocking logged; deblock benefit quantified.

12.4 Acceptance Criteria

Criterion Target
PSNR Y improvement (CIF, QP=32) ≥ 0.3 dB vs no-deblock
I9: deblock(recon_enc) == deblock(recon_dec) exact per-pixel match
BS computation unit tests (all BS=0..4 cases) 100 % pass
Strong filter arithmetic: hand-calculated 6 samples exact match
Normal filter clip arithmetic: 10 tc0 table entries exact match
No pixel clamping overflow (all outputs in [0,255]) assert in debug mode

12.5 Conformance Fixtures

Fixture Purpose Expected Output
deblock_bs4_boundary.raw intra-MB boundary, BS=4 strong filter output matches spec calculation
deblock_bs0_nochange.raw BS=0 edge pixels unchanged
deblock_qp_range.yuv QP 0,10,20,32,51 sweep alpha/beta/tc0 coverage
deblock_chroma_edge.raw chroma 8x8 boundary chroma p0/q0 update only
deblock_frame_cif_p32.yuv full CIF frame at QP=32 PSNR ≥ baseline + 0.3 dB
deblock_disabled_flag.264 disable_deblocking_filter_idc=1 no samples modified

12.6 Pitfalls

  • Edge ordering bug: vertical edges must be filtered before horizontal edges within the same MB row; reversed order introduces a 4-pixel artifact stripe. Root cause: loop order swapped. Debug: compare filtered frame with JM reference output pixel-by-pixel.
  • QP averaging across MB boundary: qp_avg = (qp_a + qp_b + 1) / 2 (rounded); using (qp_a + qp_b) / 2 causes wrong threshold at odd QP values. Signal: PSNR regression at QP=27,29,31.
  • Chroma QP mapping omission: chroma deblocking uses chroma_qp = chroma_qp_table[luma_qp]; applying luma QP directly causes wrong alpha/beta for chroma. Root cause: missing table lookup.
  • Strong filter application condition: BS=4 AND |p0-q0| < alpha/4+2 AND |p2-p0| < beta AND |q2-q0| < beta; if condition check is incomplete, strong filter applies when it should not, over-smoothing texture.
  • In-place modification order: p0/p1/p2 updates for BS=4 use pre-filter values; computing p0' before p1' with already-updated p0 gives wrong result. Use temp variables for all six samples.
  • Slice-boundary deblocking: disable_deblocking_filter_idc=2 skips filtering across slice boundaries but filters within; confusing idc=1 and idc=2 disables all deblocking silently.

12.7 Performance Concerns

  • Deblocking is applied to every MB boundary: for CIF (352×288), 22×18 = 396 MBs → 396 × 8 edges × 4 positions = 12672 filter calls per frame.
  • Strong filter (BS=4): 6 samples read + 6 writes per call; 3 filter calls per 16-pixel luma edge = 48 R/W per 16px; keep in L1 cache (96 bytes per edge).
  • Vectorize the 16-pixel horizontal edge filter: 16 consecutive bytes → single SIMD load (deferred to Phase 14 optimization; spec note only).
  • Avoid per-call threshold recomputation: cache (alpha, beta, tc0[bs]) per MB-pair QP combination (51 × 51 table, 2.6 KB).
  • Process filter in MB order (not edge order) to maximize spatial locality of plane data accesses.

12.8 Edge Cases

  • Frame boundary MBs: left column has no left neighbor → skip left vertical edge; top row has no top neighbor → skip top horizontal edge.
  • Single-MB frame (16×16): only internal luma 4x4 edges (none cross MB boundary); BS=0 everywhere; no filtering applied.
  • QP=0: alpha and beta from table are at minimum; almost no filtering; normal filter delta ≈ 0 for most edges; verify no underflow in (q0 - p0)*4 intermediate.
  • QP=51: alpha/beta at maximum; strong filter activates on nearly every edge; output may lose sharpness; PSNR may decrease at very high QP if filter over-smooths.
  • Mixed intra/inter slice: a P-slice MB adjacent to a skipped MB (BS=0 if no coeffs and |mv|<1); must not incorrectly assign BS=4.
  • disable_deblocking_filter_idc written to slice header; decoder must parse and respect it; encoder and decoder must agree — hardcode to 0 by default and assert consistency.

12.9 Failure Analysis

Failure Detection Blast Radius Recovery
Edge-order bug Horizontal stripe artifacts; PSNR does not improve Every frame Fix loop order; re-run conformance tests
Encoder/decoder deblock divergence (I9 violated) Drift accumulates; PSNR collapses after 5–10 frames Entire GOP after divergence Assert pixel equality after each frame; bisect call site
Wrong QP averaging Subtle threshold difference at odd QPs; 0.05–0.1 dB regression All odd-QP encodes Add rounding +1 in qp_avg; re-verify fixtures
Strong-filter condition missed Over-smoothing on textured edges; PSNR regression at QP < 24 High-detail frames Validate all three sub-conditions; unit test with texture fixture

12.10 Operational Notes

  • Log per-frame: BS histogram (count of BS=0..4), strong-filter activation rate, luma deblock gain (PSNR delta).
  • Metric: deblock_gain_db = PSNR-with-deblock minus PSNR-without-deblock; alert if < 0 at QP ≥ 28 (I10 direction).
  • --deblock-offset alpha_offset beta_offset CLI flags expose per-encode threshold tuning (signed byte, range −6..+6 per spec).
  • Encoder reconstruction loop must call deblock_frame before pushing to DPB; any refactor that reorders these steps violates P6; add a debug-build assertion: assert!(frame.deblocked) on DPB push.
  • vc-cli inspect --bs-map out.264 prints per-MB BS matrix per frame for visual verification of boundary strength computation.

Phase 13 — CABAC + mode decision + rate control + drivers

13.1 Objectives

  • Implement simplified CABAC in vc-entropy: binarization schemes, context model table, arithmetic coder (renormalization + bypass), flush.
  • Implement RD mode decision in vc-encoder: evaluate intra/inter/skip per macroblock via lambda*R + D and select minimum.
  • Implement basic rate control in vc-encoder: CBR/VBR QP adaptation targeting a per-frame bit budget; reaction-parameter method or simple proportional control.
  • Wire full encode driver: GOP scheduling → per-frame → per-MB mode decision → residual pipeline → entropy → NAL packetization.
  • Wire full decode driver: NAL parse → entropy decode → dequant/IDCT → prediction reconstruction → deblock → DPB output.
  • Constraint: CABAC and CAVLC must be selectable at the slice level; SliceHeader carries entropy_coding_mode_flag.

13.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

CABAC core (vc-entropy/cabac.rs):

Sub-component Details
State machine 64-state probability model; state → (LPS_range, MPS, transition_table[64][2])
Context models CtxModel { state: u8, mps: u8 }; table of ~400 contexts indexed by CtxId
Arithmetic interval (low: u32, range: u8) packed as per H.264 Annex B
Renormalization Right-shift range into [0x100,0x200), emit bits with carry propagation
Bypass coding / Flush Equiprobable symbols use no context update (simple halving); flush terminates the bin, appends stop_one_bit, byte-aligns
Binarization Unary, truncated unary, FL (fixed-length), U+EGk; map syntax element → bin string
Decode engine decode_decision(ctx), decode_bypass(), decode_terminate()

Context initialization: derive from init_value tables (as in H.264 9.3.1) using SliceQP and cabac_init_idc.

RD mode decision (vc-encoder/mode_decision.rs):

  • ModeCandidate { mb_type, bits: u32, sad: u32 } per evaluated mode.
  • Evaluate modes in order: Skip → Inter 16x16 → Inter 8x8 → Intra16x16 → IntraNxN.
  • Cost = lambda * bits + sse where lambda = pow(2, (QP - 12) / 3.0) (precomputed integer table).
  • Early termination: if Skip cost < threshold, skip further modes (P1, P5).
  • RdContext carries pre-computed neighbor availability, motion candidates, reference frames.

Rate control (vc-encoder/rate_control.rs):

  • RateController { target_bps, fps, buf_fullness, prev_qp }.
  • Frame-level: target_bits = target_bps / fps; adjust QP by delta = clip((actual - target) / step, -2, +2).
  • MB-level: optionally distribute remaining bits across remaining MBs; update after each MB.
  • Interfaces: fn frame_start(&mut self, frame_type: FrameType) -> Qp; fn frame_end(&mut self, actual_bits: u32).
  • Clamp QP to [0, 51]; enforce |QP_curr - QP_prev| <= 3 for quality stability.

Encode driver (vc-encoder/driver.rs):

  • Encoder::encode_frame(raw: &Frame) -> Vec<NalUnit> — top-level per-frame entry.
  • Steps: rate control QP → classify slice type (GOP) → per-row MB loop → mode decision → residual (transform+quant+entropy) → reconstruct → collect stats → pack NALs.
  • GopScheduler { pattern: Vec<FrameType>, period: u32, poc: i32 } drives IDR/P/B ordering.
  • Reconstruction loop: vc-deblock applied in-place on DPB reference after each frame (I9).

Decode driver (vc-decoder/driver.rs):

  • Decoder::decode_nal(nal: &NalUnit) -> Option<Frame> — per-NAL dispatch.
  • Parse SliceHeader; select CAVLC or CABAC; decode MB syntax; dequant + IDCT; intra/inter prediction from DPB; deblock; insert into DPB; output at POC. Anchors: P5 (Design Principle — RD drives mode decision), Sullivan/Wiegand and Marpe/Schwarz/Wiegand (Required Reading Papers table, RD optimization + CABAC), ADR-15 (Lagrangian RD mode decision), I9 (deblock ordering), I12 (DPB bound).

13.3 Deliverable

  • vc-entropy exposes CABAC encode/decode matching CAVLC-level residual quality.
  • vc-encoder encodes a raw YUV file to a NAL bitstream at a target bitrate.
  • vc-decoder decodes the bitstream back to YUV.
  • vc-cli encode/decode subcommands operable end-to-end.
  • Per-frame stats log: frame type, QP, bits, PSNR.

13.4 Acceptance Criteria

Check Threshold
CABAC roundtrip unit test decode(encode(symbols)) == symbols for 10 000 random residual streams
CABAC vs CAVLC bitrate delta CABAC <= CAVLC + 2% bits at same QP (or better)
Mode decision correctness Reconstructed PSNR >= CAVLC-only Phase 7 baseline at same QP
Rate control accuracy Actual bitrate within ±15% of target over 300-frame sequence
End-to-end encode+decode psnr(decoded, source) > 30 dB at QP 28, 1080p test clip
No panic on all test vectors cargo test --release + cargo bench clean

13.5 Conformance Fixtures

Fixture Purpose Expected output
fixtures/cabac_residual_roundtrip.bin Encode then decode known coefficient blocks via CABAC Exact coefficient recovery
fixtures/cabac_ctx_init_qp{28,35}.bin Context state after init at two QP values Known state tables
fixtures/rd_mode_log_foreman_qp28.csv Per-MB mode decisions on Foreman 176x144 Mode distribution sanity range
fixtures/rc_300frames_target512kbps.yuv Rate-controlled 300-frame encode Mean bitrate in [435, 589] kbps
fixtures/enc_dec_roundtrip_akiyo.yuv Full encode-decode; diff against original PSNR >= 35 dB at QP 24

13.6 Pitfalls

Pitfall Manifestation Root cause Debug signal
CABAC carry propagation Corrupted bitstream past flush; random decode errors Carry bits not propagated back through emitted bytes Trace low register at each emit; compare to reference encoder byte-by-byte
Context desync Decoder diverges at macroblock boundary Context index calculation differs between encoder/decoder for a syntax element Log (mb_addr, ctx_id, state_before) at every decode_decision; diff encoder vs decoder logs
Lambda integer overflow Mode cost wraps; wrong mode chosen silently lambda * bits exceeds u32 before cast Use u64 for intermediate RD cost; unit test at QP 51, max bits
Rate control QP oscillation Bitrate unstable; PSNR sawtooth pattern Correction gain too high; QP changes ±clip too large Plot (frame, QP, bits) time series; reduce gain constant
Reconstruction loop skipped in driver; B-frame DPB ordering in CABAC Encoder drifts, PSNR degrades over GOP; skip/direct modes use wrong reference vc-deblock not called before storing in DPB; POC-based reorder list not applied before CABAC context init Enable I2 check: hash DPB frames, compare encoder vs decoder; dump (poc, ref_list_l0, ref_list_l1) per slice, verify against H.264 sec. 8.2.4

13.7 Performance Concerns

  • Mode decision is the dominant CPU cost: evaluate cheaper modes first (Skip, then Inter 16x16) to enable early exit; amortize SAD computation. CABAC is sequential per-bin; cannot trivially parallelize within a slice — use slice-parallel encoding to scale.
  • Lambda table: precompute as [u32; 52] at startup; avoid pow in inner loop. Rate controller frame_end called once per frame; negligible cost.
  • RdContext stack-allocated per MB; avoid heap per-MB; use arena or pre-allocated scratch buffers. Context state table: 400 u8 values; fits in L1; sequential access pattern — no scatter/gather.

13.8 Edge Cases

  • QP clamped to 0: dead-zone = 0; all non-zero coefficients survive; CABAC context init tables assume QP in [0,51] — validate. All-skip slice: CABAC must still emit stop_one_bit and be byte-aligned; decoder must not stall on empty RBSP.
  • B-frame as first frame after IDR: DPB has one reference; ref_list_l1 must not index a nonexistent frame. Rate control target very low (< 50 kbps at HD): QP clamps at 51; notify caller via RateController::is_saturated().
  • Encoder encodes a single-frame sequence: GOP/B-frame logic must not reference future frames; treat as I-only. CABAC flush on exact byte boundary: must still write stop_one_bit; do not skip.

13.9 Failure Analysis

Failure Detection Blast radius Recovery
CABAC state table wrong Residual garbage on decode; PSNR collapses All frames with CABAC Re-verify table against H.264 Table 9-36; unit test context transitions
DPB drift (I2 violation) PSNR degrades monotonically over GOP All P/B frames after drift point Hash check on DPB after each frame; halt encode; emit IDR to resync
Rate controller runaway QP Bitrate far exceeds target; buffer overflow Downstream mux/transport Clamp QP delta; add hard bitrate ceiling in frame_start
Mode decision returns wrong MB type Encoder emits MB syntax decoder cannot parse Downstream slices Assert MbType consistency before CABAC encode; add decode-verify round

13.10 Operational Notes

  • Per-frame log line: FRAME poc={} type={} qp={} bits={} psnr={:.2} — machine-parseable for post-analysis; vc-cli encode --stats-csv stats.csv dumps per-frame table.
  • CABAC context state can be dumped at any MB via --cabac-trace flag; enables encoder/decoder diff. Rate control state (buf_fullness, QP history) serializable to JSON for session resume.
  • PSNR computed in vc-cli psnr subcommand separately from encode for independent verification (P10).
  • --entropy cavlc|cabac flag selects mode; default cabac; backwards-compatible with Phase 7 bitstreams when set to cavlc.

Phase 14 — hardening + conformance + perf + packing

14.1 Objectives

  • Prove I1 (bit-exact decode): any bitstream emitted by the encoder decodes to a deterministic, fixed pixel output on any platform.
  • Prove I2 (encoder/decoder reference agreement): zero drift across a full GOP; encoder DPB hashes == decoder DPB hashes at every frame.
  • Validate RD curves: PSNR vs bitrate over a standard test set is non-degenerate and monotone in QP (I10).
  • Fuzz the bitstream parser: no panic, no UB, no out-of-bounds access on arbitrary byte inputs.
  • Benchmark encode and decode throughput; document achievable fps for representative resolutions.
  • Pack the workspace into a clean release: consistent API surface, no dead code, no unwrap on untrusted paths, CI green.

14.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

Bit-exact conformance harness (tests/conformance.rs): encode a fixed test sequence (Akiyo, Foreman, silent 10 frames) at QP {24, 32, 40} with fixed seed; store golden bitstream + golden decoded YUV as hex-encoded fixtures. On each run: encode → compare bitstream byte-for-byte; decode golden bitstream → compare YUV byte-for-byte; detect non-determinism by running encode twice in the same process and comparing outputs.

DPB drift detection (tests/drift.rs): after each encoded frame, independently decode all emitted NALs from scratch into a shadow Decoder; assert sha256(encoder.dpb.frame[i]) == sha256(decoder.dpb.frame[i]) for all reference slots; integrate with a 300-frame GOP to catch long-range drift.

RD curve validation (tests/rd_curve.rs): encode the same source at QP in [18, 24, 28, 32, 36, 40, 46], record (bitrate, psnr_y); assert PSNR strictly decreases and bitrate strictly decreases as QP increases (I10); plot to artifacts/rd_curve.csv — no absolute thresholds enforced in CI, shape only.

Fuzzing (fuzz/fuzz_bitstream_parser.rs via cargo-fuzz): target vc-bitstream::parse_nal_units(input) → vc-decoder::decode_nal, corpus seeded from valid Phase 13 bitstreams and mutated with libFuzzer; assertions require no panic, no unwrap failure, no ASAN/MSAN finding, malformed input returns Err; run for minimum 1 000 000 iterations in CI (wall-clock cap 120 s). Additional fuzz targets: vc-entropy::cavlc_decode(input) and vc-entropy::cabac_decode(input).

Throughput benchmarks (benches/codec_bench.rs via Criterion):

Benchmark Input Metric
encode_i_frame_480p 480p YUV I-frame MB/s throughput
encode_p_frame_480p 480p P-frame, QP 28 MB/s throughput
decode_480p 480p bitstream MB/s throughput
motion_search_16x16 Single MB, full search cycles / MB
cavlc_encode_4x4 16 coefficients ns / block
cabac_encode_4x4 16 coefficients ns / block
idct_4x4 Single transform block ns / call
deblock_luma_row One row of MB edges ns / call

Hardening sweep: replace all unwrap() / expect() on paths reachable from untrusted bitstream input with ? or explicit error returns; vc-bitstream bounds-checks all read positions before access (return ParseError::UnexpectedEof); vc-entropy validates coeff_token table indices before lookup; vc-intra/vc-inter clamp neighbor addresses and never index outside Plane; Clippy --deny warnings gates CI.

Packing (vc-cli finalization): encode --input <yuv> --width W --height H --fps F --gop G --qp Q --bitrate B --entropy cavlc|cabac --output <bin>; decode --input <bin> --output <yuv>; psnr --ref <yuv> --dec <yuv> --width W --height H → per-frame + summary PSNR/SSIM; inspect dumps NAL unit list, slice headers, MB type histogram (--verbose adds per-MB table). Anchors: ADR-20 (fuzz hardening), ADR-23 (conformance methodology), Ribas-Corbera/Lei and Wiegand/Bruls/List/Fiscus (Required Reading Papers table, rate control + conformance), T1/T5/T9 (Threat Model rows hardened by this sweep).

14.3 Deliverable

  • cargo test --release passes all unit + integration tests, including conformance fixtures.
  • Fuzz harness runs clean for 1M iterations with zero findings.
  • Benchmark report in artifacts/bench_report.txt with throughput numbers.
  • artifacts/rd_curve.csv showing monotone PSNR vs QP for two test sequences.
  • vc-cli binary operable on raw YUV files with all four subcommands.
  • Zero unwrap on untrusted paths; Clippy --deny warnings clean.

14.4 Acceptance Criteria

Check Threshold
Bit-exact conformance Encoded bitstream byte-identical across two runs; decoded YUV byte-identical
Drift test (300 frames) max(sha256_mismatch_count) == 0 across entire GOP
RD curve monotonicity PSNR strictly decreasing in QP; bitrate strictly decreasing in QP
Fuzz (parser + entropy) 0 panics, 0 UB in 1M iterations each target
Encode throughput >= 30 fps at 480p (I+P, QP 28) on a 4-core host
PSNR sanity Decode of encoder output > 28 dB Y-PSNR at QP 36
Clippy Zero warnings with --deny warnings
Test suite cargo test --release 100% pass

fixtures/conformance/akiyo_qp24.yuv Golden decoded YUV Byte-identical decode output
