14.6 Pitfalls

Pitfall Manifestation Root cause Debug
Non-determinism from float lambda Bitstreams differ across runs or platforms f32/f64 lambda used in mode decision Replace with precomputed integer table; verify with diff on two encode outputs
DPB drift masked by PSNR PSNR looks acceptable but drift is present Low-motion content; mismatch small Use hash check (I2) rather than PSNR alone; drift compounds on scene cuts
Fuzz finds OOB in VLC table lookup ASAN/MSAN crash in cavlc_decode coeff_token index not range-checked Add bounds guard before every table dereference; replicate crash in unit test
Benchmark not representative Criterion warms up with cached data Test input fits in L2 cache for small frames Use 1080p or multi-frame input; flush cache between iterations
Golden fixture stale after algorithm fix; inspect panic on truncated NAL Conformance test always fails post-fix; hard crash in CLI Fixture not regenerated; EOF mid-RBSP without error propagation Provide --update-fixtures flag, document in CONTRIBUTING.md; propagate ParseError::UnexpectedEof through all inspect paths

14.7 Performance Concerns

  • Integer DCT (Phase 3) is the inner loop; ensure compiler auto-vectorizes 4x4 butterfly (inspect with cargo asm). Motion search (Phase 8) dominates P-frame encode; EPZS reduces candidates; SATD more costly than SAD — use SAD for coarse, SATD for refinement.
  • Deblocking (Phase 12) touches every 4x4 boundary twice (H + V); fuse luma and chroma passes to improve data reuse. CABAC is inherently serial per slice; slice-level parallelism (independent slice groups) achieves linear scaling.
  • DPB frame storage: allocate Vec<u8> once per frame slot, reuse across GOP to avoid repeated allocation. BitReader/BitWriter inner loops: ensure no heap allocation; use stack buffer of 4 KB for RBSP staging.
  • Criterion benchmarks: use black_box to prevent dead-code elimination of codec output.

14.8 Edge Cases

  • Zero-length input to decode_nal: return Err(ParseError::EmptyInput); never access data. Truncated RBSP mid-exp-Golomb: BitReader::read_ue() must return Err not hang or panic.
  • QP = 0 + all-intra: quantization step = 1; no coefficient suppressed; bitrate spikes; rate controller must not wrap.
  • Encode a sequence of 1 frame: GOP has no P/B frames; DPB empty after output; no ref_list access. Fuzz input with emulation-prevention byte at a non-canonical position: unstuffing must not corrupt payload.
  • Plane with stride > width: all read/write offsets must use stride, not width; hardening sweep must audit every plane access.
  • Decode interleaved NAL types (SPS, PPS, IDR, P, B) out of expected order: parameter set updates must be applied before slice decode; error if SPS/PPS missing.

14.9 Failure Analysis

Failure Detection Blast radius Recovery
Bit-exact regression after code change Conformance test red All bitstreams from that build Bisect commit; regenerate golden fixtures only after intentional algorithm change
Fuzz crash discovered CI fuzz job fails; crash input saved Potential panic/UB in all decoders of that build Fix parser bounds check; add crash input to crash_seeds; re-run fuzz 1M iterations clean
DPB drift in field Long-GOP video degrades visually All P/B frames after drift onset Force IDR every N seconds as mitigation; root-cause via hash log; fix encoder reconstruction
Throughput regression Criterion shows > 10% slowdown User-visible encode latency Profile with perf/flamegraph; identify hot function; revert or optimize before release
OOB plane access ASAN finding or segfault Corrupt output or crash Audit stride vs width usage across all vc-intra, vc-inter, vc-deblock plane reads

14.10 Operational Notes

  • CI pipeline stages: cargo test --release → cargo clippy --deny warnings → cargo bench --no-run → fuzz (time-capped 120 s) → conformance diff.
  • Fixture update workflow: cargo test -- --update-fixtures; commit updated fixtures/conformance/*.bin + *.yuv; require PR review.
  • --inspect output is stable text format; downstream tools may parse it; changes are breaking.
  • Benchmark artifacts committed to artifacts/ on tagged releases; regressions visible via diff.
  • PSNR tool (vc-cli psnr) usable standalone; useful for operators validating a custom encode pipeline against source.
  • Release checklist: conformance green, fuzz clean, bench report committed, CHANGELOG.md updated, crate versions bumped consistently across workspace.
  • Logging: RUST_LOG=vc_encoder=debug enables per-MB mode decision trace; vc_decoder=debug enables per-MB parse trace; vc_entropy=trace enables per-bin CABAC trace (high volume — use sparingly).

Threat Model

Trust boundaries: (1) untrusted bitstream input (network/disk/pipe) → vc-bitstream/vc-entropy parse layer is the primary crossing; (2) untrusted raw YUV input (files/pipes) → vc-color/vc-core allocation; (3) CLI operator-supplied parameters → vc-cli; (4) in-process codec library → caller; (5) build-time supply chain → Cargo dependency graph. The architecture diagram's "parse NAL/slice/MB" box is the single point where attacker-controlled bytes enter typed Rust structures; all other components receive already-validated typed data or reconstructed pixels.

Reference frame memory inside the DPB is long-lived across a GOP; corruption there propagates to all subsequent inter-predicted frames (I2 violation). The entropy layer (CAVLC/CABAC) is the second high-risk surface: desync is silent and cascading.

Trust-Boundary Summary

 [untrusted bitstream (Annex B bytes)]
          |
          v  ← PRIMARY TRUST BOUNDARY
 vc-bitstream: NAL parse + RBSP unstuff + exp-Golomb
          |
          v
 vc-entropy: CAVLC / CABAC symbol decode
          |
          v
 vc-quant + vc-transform: dequant + IDCT (typed i32 arrays)
          |
          v
 vc-intra / vc-inter: prediction from DPB (typed Plane refs)
          |
          v
 vc-deblock: in-loop filter on typed Plane
          |
          v
 vc-decoder: DPB management + output Frame
          |
          v  ← SECONDARY TRUST BOUNDARY (caller)
 caller / vc-cli: file I/O, display
  • All byte-level parsing is confined to vc-bitstream and vc-entropy.
  • After parsing, all data flows as typed Rust values with compiler-enforced bounds.
  • The DPB (Dpb) is a trust boundary internally: reference list indices must be validated before any Plane access.
  • CLI flags are validated in vc-cli before any crate-internal function is called.

Threat Table

ID Threat Attack Vector Impact Mitigation Detection Telemetry Recovery
T1 Integer overflow in exp-Golomb decode Malformed ue/se with very long leading-zero run or code-num overflowing u32 OOB BitReader advance, silent misparse, or panic Cap ue at spec max (e.g., 2^32−2); use checked_shl; return ParseError::Overflow immediately libFuzzer harness on vc-bitstream::read_ue parser.overflow_count counter Return Err; abort NAL unit; seek to next start code
T2 OOB macroblock index addressing slice_header with frame_num or first_mb_in_slice exceeding pic_size_in_mbs from SPS OOB write into Plane::data; memory corruption Validate MB index < SPS-derived pic_size_in_mbs before any MB-data access; mb_at() returns Option Property test: random MB index against known SPS decoder.mb_bounds_error counter Reject slice; output error-concealed frame
T3 DPB reference list poisoning B/P slice ref_idx > dpb.len(), or ref_idx points to an un-marked frame Inter predictor reads wrong reference pixels; PSNR collapses; potential OOB Validate ref_idx < ref_list.len() and frame is marked as short/long-term reference before MC fetch Unit tests for all DPB boundary states; fuzz slice headers decoder.dpb_ref_error counter Skip slice; output grey concealment; continue from next IDR
T4 Emulation-prevention bypass Crafted byte sequence containing 0x000003 positioned to de-sync RBSP unstuffer Parser reads wrong byte offsets; incorrect coeff or MV data; frame corruption RBSP unstuffing runs as a dedicated pre-pass before all parsing; state machine handles 0x000001/0x000002 boundary cases Roundtrip fuzz enforcing I7; explicit test with synthetic 0x000003 sequences bitstream.rbsp_error counter Reject NAL unit; emit ParseError::EmulationPrevention
T5 Allocation bomb via oversized SPS SPS width=65535, height=65535 → stride×height = ~4 GB Vec<u8> OOM; process kill; DoS Cap pic_width/height to MAX_SUPPORTED_PIC_SIZE (configurable, default 4096×4096); validate before any Vec allocation; use checked_mul for stride computation Reject before alloc; test SPS with max legal dims sps.dimension_rejected counter Return Err(ParseError::DimensionLimit); no allocation made
T6 MV pointing outside padded reference plane Crafted MV + block position → address outside Plane extent even after edge extension OOB read in vc-inter MC fetch; garbage prediction data Clip MV to [−MAX_MV_RANGE, +MAX_MV_RANGE]; allocate ref planes with MAX_FILTER_HALFWIDTH padding (e.g., 16 px) on all edges; MC always reads within padded area Unit test MV at ±MAX_MV; fuzz MV field of slice data motion.mv_clipped counter Clamp; continue; no panic; prediction quality degrades gracefully
T7 Quantized coefficient level overflow Large level values combined with QP=0 dequant → i32 overflow in IDCT butterfly Silent wrong reconstruction; potential UB if unchecked Clip level to spec-defined range (±255 for 4×4 baseline, ±2047 for high profile) before dequant; use saturating_mul in IDCT butterfly Round-trip tests at QP=0 with max-magnitude coefficients quant.coeff_clipped counter Saturate; output degraded but valid; no UB
T8 CABAC arithmetic state desync after bit error Single corrupted bit corrupts context probability tables or value range All subsequent symbols in slice wrong; cascade of wrong MBs CABAC decoder checks for illegal state transitions → abort slice on first undefined state; slice data length validation before decoding CABAC state reachability tests; fuzz CABAC bitstream entropy.cabac_abort counter Abort slice at first desync; conceal remaining MBs; continue at next IDR
T9 CAVLC infinite loop / hang total_zeros or run_before table miss with crafted input → unbounded loop in VLC search CPU DoS; process hang Look-up tables cover all valid codewords; any input not in table returns Err immediately; no loops in table lookup Code-coverage on all CAVLC table branches + fuzz entropy.cavlc_table_miss counter Return Err(EntropyError::TableMiss); reject macroblock
T10 Supply-chain / Cargo dependency confusion Malicious transitive crate introduced via cargo update or typosquat Arbitrary code execution at build or runtime Lock Cargo.lock; enforce cargo deny (license + advisory); minimize dependencies (prefer zero-dep crates); run cargo audit in CI CI cargo-audit gate; advisory database subscription Advisory alert in CI log Pin Cargo.lock; rotate affected crate immediately
T11 Operator parameter misconfiguration QP=0 on a long, high-motion sequence → multi-GB output; b_frames > gop_size → encoder assert Storage exhaustion; encode crash Validate all CLI params before codec init: QP ∈ [0,51], b_frames < gop_size, output path writable and has headroom, width/height even vc-cli param validator runs before opening output file cli.param_error log Reject with actionable error message; no partial output
T12 Encoder reconstruction / decoder mismatch (drift) Encoder predicts from original pixels instead of reconstructed; deblock applied in encoder but not decoder (or vice versa) I2 violated; PSNR degrades monotonically over GOP Single shared compute_bs() and deblock apply in both encoder loop and decoder; encoder never reads source for prediction after Phase 2 drift_check binary: encode N frames, decode bitstream, compare pixel-exact encoder.drift_delta_db metric (must be 0) Not a runtime recovery — fail CI; fix violating code path
T13 File-descriptor exhaustion Encoding thousands of frames without closing intermediate file handles ENFILE; encode stalls mid-stream All YUV I/O uses RAII wrappers; no persistent temp files; explicit test with ulimit -n 32 Integration test with low fd limit io.open_fd_count gauge Graceful Err(IoError::TooManyOpenFiles); close output file cleanly
T14 POC counter wrap-around Encoding beyond ~2B frames; POC arithmetic wraps i32 Frame reordering breaks; DPB marks wrong reference Wrapping POC arithmetic per H.264 spec (modular diff); tested at POC near i32::MAX − max_gop_size Unit test POC arithmetic at boundary values encoder.poc_wrap_event counter Transparent wrap; correct reference marking maintained
T15 Bitstream replay / substitution Attacker feeds previously recorded valid bitstream to a stateful decoder Decoder accepts stale content as current; no authentication Out of scope for codec library; authentication is a transport-layer concern — — Document: consumers must authenticate streams at transport layer
T16 Deblocking filter boundary out-of-bounds Incorrect edge index causes filter to read/write beyond Plane::data Memory corruption in reference frame Boundary checks on all 4×4 edge indices before filter application; filter width validated against plane dimensions Unit test: deblock 16×16 block at frame corner deblock.bounds_error counter Reject macroblock; no filter applied
T17 Chroma subsampling dimension mismatch Caller passes YUV with luma 351×287 (odd dimensions) Chroma plane allocation off by one; misaligned U/V strides vc-color validates width/height are even before subsampling; vc-cli enforces even dimensions Param validator in vc-cli; unit test odd-dimension input color.dimension_error counter Reject with Err(ColorError::OddDimension); no partial alloc
T18 Hadamard transform DC coefficient saturation 16×16 Hadamard DC block of 16 saturated coefficients with QP=0 i32 accumulator overflow in Hadamard butterfly Use i64 intermediate accumulator for Hadamard sum; saturate to i32 range before output Unit test: all-max input block through Hadamard at QP=0 transform.hadamard_sat counter Saturate to i32::MAX/MIN; no UB

Failure Modes & Recovery

Scope: all failure classes observable during encode, decode, or test. Each row covers a distinct behavioral failure, not a specific bug. Cross-reference threats from the Threat Model where applicable.

Failure Taxonomy

Class Symptoms Root Causes Detection Automatic Recovery Manual Recovery Operator Actions Forensic Artifacts Long-term Mitigation
Bitstream parse failure ParseError returned; frame skipped; decode output stops Malformed NAL header; exp-Golomb overflow; truncated RBSP; wrong slice_type Error log with NAL byte offset + error variant Skip to next Annex-B start code (0x000001); output grey concealment frame; continue Re-encode source; replace corrupt file segment vc-cli inspect <file> to dump NAL structure Hex dump of offending NAL unit + byte offset Fuzz regression (T1, T4); stricter upfront validation
DPB reference underflow DpbError::RefNotFound; inter slice undecodable; output frozen or grey Missing IDR at stream start; dropped packets; wrong ref_idx in slice header DpbError counter spike; PSNR drop to near 0 Treat slice as forced-intra if ref unavailable; output concealment frame Re-ingest stream from last IDR; request upstream to send IDR Check for packet loss upstream; force IDR injection Last decoded POC; DPB state snapshot at error Error-resilience mode: IDR every N seconds; T3 mitigation
Encoder/decoder drift (I2) Encoder recon diverges from decoder output; PSNR degrades monotonically over GOP Deblock mismatch; float arithmetic; encoder predicting from source not recon assert_eq!(enc_recon, dec_out) per frame; per-GOP drift metric > 0 None — drift must not occur; fail-fast in CI Bisect to commit introducing divergence; isolate to MB/frame Run drift-check binary; compare per-MB pixel arrays Diff of enc_recon vs dec_out plane arrays; commit hash P2/P6 enforced; CI drift check on every commit; no float (P3)
Integer overflow / panic Thread panic; encode/decode aborts with backtrace Unchecked arithmetic in IDCT butterfly; stride computation; MV arithmetic; coefficient scale RUST_BACKTRACE=1 panic log; cargo-fuzz crash file Rust panics propagate as process abort or caught by fuzz harness Reproduce with minimal bitstream; add checked_*; add to corpus File issue with minimal reproducing bitstream fragment Panic backtrace + input bitstream checked_* / saturating_* everywhere; fuzz CI; static analysis
Entropy desync (CAVLC) Garbage coefficients after a valid MB; all subsequent MBs wrong; PSNR collapses One bit error in transmission; off-by-one in run_before table; wrong coeff_token CAVLC table miss counter; subsequent MB types illegal Abort current slice; conceal remaining MBs in slice Inspect entropy log; confirm I6 roundtrip in isolation entropy-error log + slice-start byte offset Raw RBSP bytes + context state at failure Roundtrip fuzz (I6); exhaustive VLC table coverage tests
Entropy desync (CABAC) All symbols after a valid MB corrupted; CABAC state reaches undefined transition Single bit error; off-by-one in renormalization; context index out of range Illegal state transition in arithmetic coder Abort slice at first undefined state; output concealment Re-encode; reproduce with fuzz harness Identify CABAC state at failure; check binarization scheme CABAC internal state dump (value, range, context table) State reachability tests; fuzz CABAC paths separately
Memory exhaustion (OOM) Process killed; encode stalls mid-GOP; Rust allocator panic Allocation bomb via large SPS (T5); DPB holding too many frames; leaked frame buffers OOM killer log; RSS metric > threshold Reject after SPS validation (T5 cap); DPB frame count = f(SPS max_ref_frames) Reduce DPB size; lower input resolution; stream-restart Monitor RSS; set container memory limit Last successful POC; RSS time series Dimension cap (T5); DPB bounded by SPS; frame pool with fixed capacity
Rate-control divergence Bitrate >> target; QP oscillates at extremes; output file unexpectedly large or tiny RC feedback gain too high; first-frame QP seed wrong; lambda miscalibrated Bits-per-frame metric; QP-vs-target scatter plot; output file size anomaly Clamp QP to [QP_MIN, QP_MAX] per frame; reset RC integrator after each IDR Re-encode with conservative RC params; reduce max_qp_delta Inspect per-frame bits + QP log; compare RD curve to expected Per-frame QP + bits log (P10); target vs actual bitrate time series Validate RC on standard test sequences before release; P5 lambda calibration
Deblocking artifact Visible block edges in output; PSNR regression after filter enabled Wrong boundary-strength computation; incorrect alpha/beta table index; BS applied at wrong granularity PSNR with filter ON vs OFF; visual diff of test frame None (silent quality degradation; no crash) Isolate with single-MB test vector; compare BS to H.264 Table 8-16/17 Diff filtered vs unfiltered plane Per-edge BS values; QP used; luma/chroma plane Test BS computation exhaustively; compare to JM reference output
GOP / POC ordering error B-frame output before its references; frame timestamps non-monotone; DPB ref-list wrong POC ordering bug; DPB reference list build error; B-frame cadence misconfigured Output frame POC sequence non-ascending; DpbError counter Force IDR; reset DPB Re-encode with simple IPPP GOP; diagnose Phase 11 DPB logic Check frame-type sequence in vc-cli inspect output POC + DPB state snapshot at failure Phase 11 DPB unit tests; POC boundary tests; test with all GOP patterns
Intra prediction neighbor unavailability Corruption at frame edges or slice boundaries; wrong modes selected at slice start Incorrect availability check for top/left neighbors at MB (0,0) or slice boundary Property test: encode row 0 MBs; compare to known-good prediction Use DC fallback when neighbors unavailable (spec-mandated behavior) Verify availability mask logic against H.264 clause 8.3 Wrong output pixels at frame boundary Raw prediction block vs expected Exhaustive availability tests for all 4×4 + 16×16 border conditions
CLI / I/O error Encode/decode exits immediately; partial output file left File not found; wrong YUV dimensions; output disk full; bad frame count Exit code ≠ 0; error message on stderr No output written if dimensions fail validation; output file closed and removed on error Verify input dimensions; free disk space; check frame count vc-cli psnr sanity on a short clip CLI invocation string; OS error code; output file size Validate all params and I/O handles before opening output; RAII cleanup on drop
Sub-pixel interpolation boundary error Visual glitches at frame boundaries for fast-motion content; slight PSNR loss MC fetch from quarter-pel position near frame edge without sufficient padding Unit test: 6-tap filter at edge position; compare to JM reference Edge extension (border replication) fills padding region before filter Verify padding width ≥ MAX_FILTER_HALFWIDTH (3 px for 6-tap) Per-frame artifact detection (SSIM outlier) Affected MV + block position; reference frame region Phase 9 MC boundary tests; static assertion on padding width

Operational Readiness

Metrics Strategy

Emit all metrics as structured key-value pairs. In library mode, callers receive a FrameStats struct per frame. In CLI mode, JSON lines to stderr with --stats flag.

Metric Name Type Source Crate Granularity Alert Threshold
frame.psnr_luma_db gauge (f64) vc-encoder / vc-cli psnr per frame < 30 dB on non-synthetic content
frame.psnr_chroma_db gauge (f64) vc-encoder / vc-cli psnr per frame < 28 dB
frame.bits counter (u64) vc-entropy per frame > 3× target frame bits
frame.qp_avg gauge (f32) vc-encoder per frame > 45 (quality floor)
frame.mode_dist.intra_pct gauge (f32) vc-encoder per frame < 5% on expected-motion sequence (skip regression)
frame.mode_dist.skip_pct gauge (f32) vc-encoder per frame > 80% on first P-frame (motion search broken)
gop.drift_psnr_delta_db gauge (f64) drift-check per GOP > 0.01 dB (any drift — I2 violation)
decoder.parse_error_count counter (u64) vc-bitstream per stream > 0
decoder.dpb_ref_error_count counter (u64) vc-decoder per stream > 0
entropy.cavlc_error_count counter (u64) vc-entropy per stream > 0
entropy.cabac_abort_count counter (u64) vc-entropy per stream > 0
encoder.throughput_fps gauge (f64) vc-cli bench per run < 1 fps on CIF (perf regression)
decoder.throughput_fps gauge (f64) vc-cli bench per run < 30 fps on CIF
process.rss_bytes gauge (u64) OS / metrics hook per encode/decode > 512 MB for 1080p input
dpb.occupancy gauge (usize) vc-decoder / vc-encoder per frame > DPB_MAX − 1
motion.mv_clipped_count counter (u64) vc-motion per frame > 5% of MBs (unusual; investigate)
quant.coeff_clipped_count counter (u64) vc-quant per frame > 0 (unexpected for conformant inputs)

Tracing Strategy

  • Instrumentation crate: tracing with tracing-subscriber.
  • Span hierarchy: encode_gop[gop_idx] → encode_frame[poc, frame_type] → encode_mb[mb_idx] (sampled 1% in production; 100% in debug builds).
  • Decoder: decode_nal[nal_type, offset] → decode_slice[slice_type, poc] → decode_mb[mb_idx] (same sampling).
  • Span fields propagated: poc, frame_type, qp, mb_type, gop_idx.
  • Entropy spans: cavlc_encode_mb / cabac_encode_mb at DEBUG level only (high frequency).
  • Async context: codec is synchronous; spans are thread-local; no async executor.
  • Export: tracing-opentelemetry for CI integration; plain fmt subscriber for CLI.
  • Performance budget for tracing: < 2% overhead in release mode at frame-level granularity.

Structured Logging

  • Format: JSON lines via tracing-subscriber::fmt::json(); one object per log event.
  • Mandatory fields: ts (ISO-8601), level, target (crate::module), poc, event, value.
  • Optional fields: mb_idx, mv, mode, rd_cost, qp, bits.
  • Log levels:
    • ERROR: parse failures, arithmetic panics, DPB errors, drift violations.
    • WARN: clipped MVs, clipped coefficients, DPB near-full, RC QP extremes.
    • INFO: per-frame summary (poc, frame_type, bits, psnr_luma, qp_avg).
    • DEBUG: per-MB mode decision, MV, RD cost.
    • TRACE: entropy symbol streams (CI only; not for production).
  • No raw pixel data in logs. No PII. Frame content is opaque to the log layer.

Dashboard Design

Eight recommended panels (Grafana or equivalent):

Panel X-axis Y-axis / Series Purpose
RD Curve Bitrate (kbps) PSNR luma (dB) Codec quality; one series per QP sweep per test sequence
Per-frame PSNR POC PSNR luma + chroma Scene-cut and RC health
Per-frame bits + target POC bits, target_bits Rate control tracking
Per-frame QP POC QP avg Rate control stability
Mode distribution POC stacked: I%, P%, B%, skip% Motion search health; encoder regression
Drift delta GOP index drift_psnr_delta I2 monitor (should be flat at 0)
Throughput Run timestamp fps (encode + decode) Performance regression
Error counters Run timestamp parse_error, dpb_ref_error, cavlc_error, cabac_abort Quality gate

Alerting Philosophy

  • Alert on statistical regression, not absolute thresholds: compare metric to 7-day rolling mean for the same test sequence and codec configuration.
  • Page on: drift_psnr_delta > 0 (I2 violation); any parse/dpb/entropy error count > 0; PSNR regression > 0.5 dB at same QP vs baseline; throughput regression > 20%.
  • Warn on: QP > 45; RSS > 400 MB; skip% < 3% on motion-heavy sequence; mv_clipped > 2% of MBs.
  • Silence: minor bitrate fluctuation within ±5%; PSNR jitter < 0.1 dB.
  • No alert fatigue: each alert must be actionable and map to a runbook.

SLO Definitions

SLO ID Description Target Measurement
SLO-1 Bit-exact decode (I1) 100% CI: decode same bitstream on two independent release builds → byte-identical output, every commit
SLO-2 Drift-free encoder (I2) 0 drift across 300-frame GOP CI drift-check binary on foreman_cif.yuv and bus_cif.yuv
SLO-3 Entropy roundtrip (I6, I7) 100% on full fuzz corpus CAVLC + CABAC roundtrip; BitWriter/BitReader roundtrip; nightly fuzz
SLO-4 PSNR floor at QP=28 ≥ 35 dB luma on foreman_cif.yuv CI benchmark; measured by vc-cli psnr
SLO-5 Parser fuzz: no panic 0 panics on 10M random inputs cargo-fuzz nightly run on vc-bitstream + vc-entropy targets
SLO-6 Encode throughput (CIF, I+P, QP=28) ≥ 5 fps on reference CI machine vc-cli bench Phase 14
SLO-7 Decode throughput (CIF) ≥ 30 fps on reference CI machine vc-cli bench Phase 14
SLO-8 PSNR monotonicity (I10) Decreasing QP never decreases PSNR on fixed source Property test across QP 20–45 on foreman_cif

Incident Response Procedure

  1. Alert fires — identify triggering metric and associated runbook from the table below.
  2. Triage — run vc-cli inspect <file> to dump NAL/slice structure; run vc-cli psnr to quantify quality delta; check error counter log.
  3. Isolate — bisect to the commit introducing the regression; reduce to minimal reproducing bitstream (single frame or single MB if possible).
  4. Contain (production decode) — gate on IDR presence; skip degraded slice; output grey concealment frame; log poc and error type.
  5. Root-cause — match failure to Failure Mode table; attach forensic artifacts (bitstream fragment, backtrace, metrics snapshot, drift diff).
  6. Fix — implement fix; add minimal repro to fuzz corpus and property-test suite; verify CI green.
  7. Post-mortem — update Threat Model if new attack vector discovered; update SLO if SLO definition was wrong; update runbook.

Runbooks

Runbook ID Trigger Key Diagnostic Steps Resolution Path
RB-01 parse_error_count > 0 vc-cli inspect → find NAL byte offset → check emulation-prevention → add to fuzz corpus Harden vc-bitstream parse; add regression test
RB-02 drift_psnr_delta > 0 Run drift-check binary per frame → find first diverging frame → isolate to intra/inter/deblock path → check for float arithmetic Remove float; unify deblock apply path in encoder loop and decoder
RB-03 PSNR regression > 0.5 dB Bisect commit; compare RD curve; check QP table, intra-mode, deblock param changes Revert or fix the regressing commit; add RD regression test
RB-04 RSS > 512 MB or OOM Check SPS dimensions vs T5 cap; check DPB frame count vs max_ref_frames; check for frame buffer leak Enforce dimension cap; bound DPB; fix leak with RAII
RB-05 Throughput regression > 20% cargo flamegraph on failing benchmark; identify hot path; check motion search inner loop; check allocation in encode_mb Optimize hot path; add benchmark to CI performance gate
RB-06 cavlc_error_count > 0 Run CAVLC unit test on failing MB residual vector; check table completeness for total_zeros/run_before Add missing table entry; add regression test; add to fuzz corpus
RB-07 cabac_abort_count > 0 Dump CABAC state at abort (value, range, ctx_idx); check binarization scheme matches spec Fix state transition; add state reachability test
RB-08 dpb_ref_error_count > 0 Dump DPB state log at error POC; verify IDR presence; check reference list construction in Phase 11 Fix DPB marking or ref_list build; add POC boundary test
RB-09 mv_clipped_count > 5% Check MV range against sequence content; confirm quarter-pel MV units used consistently Verify MV is in quarter-pel units throughout vc-motion and vc-inter; fix unit mismatch
RB-10 Fuzz crash file found Reproduce: cargo fuzz run <target> <crashfile>; minimize with cargo fuzz tmin Fix root cause; add minimized input to regression corpus

CI Pipeline Topology

 PR / push
    |
    +-- cargo fmt --check
    +-- cargo clippy -- -D warnings
    +-- cargo test (unit + integration, debug build)
    |       includes: color roundtrip, transform roundtrip, entropy roundtrip (I6/I7),
    |                 drift-check (I2), SLO-4 PSNR, SLO-8 PSNR monotonicity
    +-- cargo test --release (benchmark smoke: encode + decode throughput)
    +-- cargo deny check (license + advisory — T10)
    +-- cargo audit (CVE database)
    |
    nightly only:
    +-- cargo fuzz run vc_bitstream_fuzz (10M inputs — SLO-5)
    +-- cargo fuzz run vc_entropy_cavlc_fuzz
    +-- cargo fuzz run vc_entropy_cabac_fuzz
    +-- cargo fuzz run vc_decoder_fuzz
    +-- RD benchmark: encode foreman_cif + bus_cif at QP 20/28/36/45;
        compare PSNR + bits to stored baseline (fail on > 0.5 dB regression)
  • All CI steps run on a fixed reference machine (same CPU, same OS image) for throughput comparability.
  • Fuzz crash files archived as CI artifacts; replayed in every subsequent nightly run.
  • Drift-check binary encodes 300 frames, decodes the bitstream, asserts pixel-exact match.
  • Test vectors in tests/vectors/ are hash-verified on checkout; never modified in-place.

Deployment Topology

  • Library mode: vc-encoder and vc-decoder linked as Rust crates into host application; no daemon, no network surface, no persistent process state; threat surface = API input validation only.
  • CLI mode: single static binary vc-cli; input from files or stdin pipe; output to files or stdout; runs as invoking user; no privilege escalation; no network sockets.
  • CI benchmark node: dedicated machine with fixed CPU governor (performance); pinned CPU affinity; benchmark results written to JSON artifact and compared to stored baseline.
  • Fuzz nodes: nightly cargo-fuzz runs in separate CI job; crash files archived as CI artifacts; fuzz corpus stored in fuzz/corpus/ tracked in version control.
  • No GPU, no kernel module, no shared memory IPC, no daemon, no network listener in scope for v1.

Canary / Rolling Upgrade / Rollback

  • Canary: before promoting a new minor version, run it on 10% of the CI test-sequence corpus (randomly sampled); compare PSNR and throughput to the current release; promote only if both metrics within tolerance.
  • Rolling upgrade (library consumers): semver minor bump is backward-compatible; consumers update Cargo.lock independently; no coordination required.
  • Rollback: pin previous version in consumer Cargo.lock; no in-process state persists between invocations (codec is stateless per session); rollback is instantaneous.
  • Migration (bitstream format change): version is signalled in SPS profile/level or a custom SEI NAL; old bitstreams remain decodable by old decoder; new syntax elements are gated behind a feature flag until stable.

Backup and Test-Vector Management

  • Encoded .264 / .vc bitstream files are the canonical output artifact; source YUV files (stored in tests/vectors/, tracked via Git LFS) are the authoritative input backup.
  • DPB state is ephemeral per decode session; no persistence across invocations; no backup needed.
  • Test-vector corpus: tests/vectors/ under version control (Git LFS for large YUV files); hash-checked on CI checkout.
  • Fuzz corpus: fuzz/corpus/<target>/ tracked in CI artifact storage; retained indefinitely; replayed on every nightly fuzz run.
  • Re-encoding from source YUV is the restore path; decoder output from a given bitstream is deterministic (I1) so re-decode is also a valid restore.

Compatibility & Versioning

Security Properties Summary

Property Mechanism Verified By
Memory safety Rust ownership + borrow checker; no unsafe in hot paths Compile-time; CI with #![forbid(unsafe_code)] on core crates
No integer UB checked_* / saturating_* in all arithmetic on untrusted data Fuzz + property tests
No buffer overread Plane::data access via mb_at() returning Option; padded ref frames Fuzz (T1–T6); MIRI in CI (debug)
No DoS via CPU spin All VLC table lookups are O(1) (no search loops) Code review; fuzz timeout
No DoS via allocation SPS dimension cap before any Vec allocation Unit test with MAX+1 dims
No silent drift Drift-check binary enforces I2 on every commit SLO-2 CI gate
Dependency integrity Cargo.lock pinned; cargo deny; cargo audit CI gate (T10)

Scope

These guarantees apply to: (1) the .264-style NAL-unit bitstream format emitted by vc-encoder and consumed by vc-decoder; (2) the public Rust API surface of vc-core, vc-encoder, vc-decoder, vc-entropy, vc-bitstream, and vc-cli; (3) the raw YUV 4:2:0 planar I/O format used by vc-cli. Internal module APIs, Dpb in-memory state, and per-frame reconstruction buffers carry no compatibility obligation.

Wire / Bitstream Compatibility

Guarantee Scope Conditions Policy
Forward bitstream compat Bitstream from encoder vN decodable by decoder vN+1 No change to syntax profile/level fields Maintained across all minor and patch bumps
Backward bitstream compat Decoder vN decodes bitstream from encoder vN−1 Minor version; same profile/level Maintained; documented when relaxed
Cross-build bit-exact decode (I1) Same bitstream → identical pixels on any conformant build Integer-only transforms (P3) Always enforced; never relaxed; tested by SLO-1
Encoder output reproducibility Same source + params → same bitstream Same CPU, OS, Cargo build Not guaranteed (RC, mode decision may vary); only decoder output is bit-exact
Profile/level signalling SPS profile_idc / level_idc correctly reflect feature use New features (CABAC, B-frames) increase profile/level Parsers check profile before using advanced syntax; unknown profile → Err(UnsupportedProfile)

Storage / Format Compatibility

Format Stability Class Notes
NAL unit framing (Annex B 0x000001 start codes) Frozen Governed by H.264 spec; vc-bitstream must remain conformant
RBSP syntax (exp-Golomb field order per H.264 Tables 7-1..7-4) Stable Changes require major semver bump + migration note
Raw YUV 4:2:0 planar I/O (width × height × 1.5 bytes per frame) Stable Standard interop format; dimensions and frame-count carried out-of-band via CLI flags
Plane / Frame in-memory layout Unstable (private) Not serialized; consumers use the public Frame constructor
Fuzz corpus format Stable within a fuzz target Corpus files are raw bitstream fragments; remain valid as long as the parse API is stable
Test-vector YUV files Stable (immutable, hash-pinned) Added but never modified in tests/vectors/; hash verified on CI

Schema / Type Evolution

Type Evolution Policy
NalUnit, SliceHeader, Macroblock Fields added as Option<T> in minor versions; existing fields never removed or reordered in minor/patch
FrameType, MbType, IntraMode #[non_exhaustive] on all public enums; new variants added in minor versions; callers must handle _ arm
Qp(u8) Wire representation fixed (0..51); internal newtype; no serialization
MotionVector { x: i16, y: i16 } Field types fixed; quarter-pel unit convention documented and stable
BitWriter / BitReader Public API (ue/se read/write, start-code search) stable across minor versions; internal state is private
Dpb, RefList In-memory only; no serialization; no compatibility obligation; may be restructured in any version
FrameStats (metrics struct) Fields added in minor versions; existing fields never removed; new fields default to 0/None

API Stability

Public Surface Stable Since Policy
vc-core public types (Frame, Plane, Macroblock, Qp, MotionVector) Phase 1 freeze Semver-minor for additions; major for removal or incompatible change
vc-encoder::encode_gop() / encode_frame() entry points Phase 6 (intra) Signatures locked at minor; behavior changes documented in CHANGELOG
vc-decoder::decode_stream() / decode_nal() entry points Phase 6 Same as above
vc-entropy CAVLC/CABAC encode/decode APIs Phase 7 (CAVLC) / Phase 13 (CABAC) Symbol type signatures locked at minor
vc-bitstream BitWriter/BitReader public API Phase 2 Stable; internal state private
vc-cli subcommand flags Phase 14 New flags added with old-behavior default; removed only on major bump with deprecation period
Crate-internal module APIs Never May change in any version; not part of semver surface

Migration Guarantees

Scenario Guarantee
Phase 6 intra-only bitstream decoded by Phase 14 full decoder I-frame decode path unchanged; guaranteed decodable
CAVLC bitstream decoded after CABAC added (Phase 13) entropy_coding_mode_flag=0 in slice header selects CAVLC; CABAC addition does not break CAVLC bitstreams
P-only bitstream decoded after B-frame support added (Phase 11) B-frame support is additive; P-only bitstreams remain decodable
QP table or transform scaling change If changed, old bitstreams decode with old-table semantics (table version inferred from SPS profile/level per spec)
API breaking change Old API deprecated one minor cycle; #[deprecated] applied; removed on next major bump; migration note in CHANGELOG

Deprecation Policy

  • Deprecated items annotated #[deprecated(since = "X.Y.0", note = "use Z instead")].
  • Deprecated API maintained for one full major version cycle (e.g., 1.Y.0 → 2.0.0).
  • Removal announced in CHANGELOG at point of deprecation; confirmed at major bump.
  • A deprecated API that decodes a bitstream format is never removed without a migration tool or re-encode path.
  • No silent behavior changes: if semantics change, the old symbol is deprecated and a new symbol introduced.

Semantic-Versioning Policy

Change Type Bump
Bug fix with no API or bitstream change Patch (0.0.Z)
New feature; backward-compatible API addition; new encoder optimization (RD, RC improvement) Minor (0.Y.0)
Breaking API change; breaking bitstream format change; public type removal or rename Major (X.0.0)
Security fix with no API change Patch
Security fix requiring API change Minor or Major depending on scope
New SPS profile/level support Minor (decoder handles new profile; encoder can emit)

Rolling-Upgrade Guarantees

Scenario Guarantee Condition
Decoder vN+1 decodes bitstream from encoder vN Yes Minor bump; same or lower profile/level
Encoder vN+1 + decoder vN Yes for features within supported profile; No for new syntax elements not understood by old decoder Gate new syntax behind feature flag; advertise in SPS
Mixed-version CI gate Always: run previous-release decoder against HEAD encoder output Must produce same pixel output as same-version decode
Library consumer pins Cargo.lock Consumer controls upgrade timing; no forced breaking change on minor bump —

Interoperability with External Decoders

  • The bitstream targets H.264 Baseline / Main profile semantics; a conformant external decoder (e.g., FFmpeg libavcodec, JM reference decoder) must accept bitstreams emitted by vc-encoder.
  • Conformance tested in Phase 14 by decoding vc-encoder output with an external decoder and comparing PSNR (not pixel-exact — external decoders may use float for some operations).
  • vc-decoder must accept bitstreams from a conformant external encoder (e.g., x264 at Baseline profile) without panic.
  • Non-conformant or profile-exceeding syntax (e.g., field coding, FMO, ASO) → Err(UnsupportedSyntax); never panic.
  • Quarter-pel interpolation filter: H.264 normative 6-tap luma filter; chroma 1/8-pel bilinear; any conformant decoder implements identically, ensuring I1 across implementations.

Cross-Reference: Types Defined in Domain Model and Core Types (Sections 8/9)

Type Bitstream Presence Compatibility Class
NalUnit { nal_type, rbsp } Every byte emitted to file Frozen by Annex B framing spec
SliceHeader { slice_type, frame_num, poc, ref_list, qp } Per-slice RBSP Stable; fields added as Optional only
Macroblock { mb_type, qp, parts, residual, cbp } Per-MB syntax elements Stable; #[non_exhaustive] on MbType
MotionVector { x: i16, y: i16 } MVD in slice data Fixed; quarter-pel unit invariant
Qp(u8) QP delta in slice/MB header Fixed range 0..51; never serialized standalone
`TransformBlock { coeffs: [i32; 16 64] }` Quantized levels in entropy coder
Dpb / RefList Not serialized; reconstructed from bitstream No compatibility obligation
Frame { planes: [Plane;3], poc, frame_type } Output artifact (YUV file) YUV 4:2:0 planar format stable; poc is display metadata only

Versioning of Test Vectors and Fuzz Corpus

Artifact Storage Versioning Stability
tests/vectors/*.yuv (source) Git LFS SHA-256 hash pinned in tests/vectors/SHA256SUMS Immutable after initial commit; new sequences added as new files
tests/vectors/*.264 (reference bitstreams) Git LFS Hash pinned Regenerated only on major version bumps; old files retained
fuzz/corpus/vc_bitstream_fuzz/ CI artifact storage + local fuzz/corpus/ dir No hash pinning needed; inputs are opaque bytes Grow-only; never remove inputs from corpus
fuzz/corpus/vc_entropy_cavlc_fuzz/ Same Same Same
RD baseline JSON (ci/rd_baseline.json) Version-controlled Updated manually on intentional quality improvement; diff reviewed in PR Update requires explicit PR with measured before/after PSNR table

Testing & Verification Strategy

Unit Testing

Layer What How
vc-color RGB→YUV→RGB roundtrip proptest: random RGB, convert both ways, assert max rounding delta ≤ 1 per channel
vc-transform I3: inverse_dct(forward_dct(x)) == x proptest: random i16 block, forward then inverse, exact equality
vc-transform Hadamard DC transform correctness fixed-pattern blocks, compare against reference hand-computed values
vc-quant I5: quant error ≤ step/2 proptest: random coeff + QP in 0..51, assert dequant(quant(c)) - c ≤ step/2
vc-quant Chroma QP mapping table exhaustive: all 52 luma QP values map to expected chroma QP
vc-intra I4: prediction causality unit: verify predictor reads only x<current_col or y<current_row neighbor positions
vc-intra 9-mode 4×4 predictor outputs frozen golden bytes for each mode on a known 4×4 block with seeded neighbors
vc-intra 16×16 DC/H/V/plane predictor known macroblock border, compare prediction block byte-for-byte
vc-entropy I6 CAVLC roundtrip proptest: random coeff run (up to 16 non-zero), encode then decode, exact equality
vc-entropy I6 CABAC roundtrip proptest: symbol sequences 0..255, binary arithmetic encode+decode, exact equality
vc-entropy CABAC context reset state machine: after CABAC flush, probability states reset to init; no leak across slices
vc-bitstream I7 exp-Golomb ue/se roundtrip proptest: random u32/i32, write then read, exact equality
vc-bitstream Emulation-prevention insert+strip inject 0x000000/0x000001/0x000003 patterns, verify 0x000003 insertion and removal lossless
vc-bitstream NAL unit framing serialize NalUnit, parse back, verify nal_type and RBSP content unchanged
vc-motion SAD / SATD correctness hand-verified reference values for small block pairs; compare against brute-force scan
vc-motion Diamond search finds known MV construct artificial frame pair where true MV is known, assert search returns it
vc-deblock Boundary-strength table enumerate MB-pair scenarios (I/P/skip, coded coeffs, MV diff), compare BS against spec table
vc-deblock I9: determinism same reconstructed MB → identical filtered output, two calls produce same bytes

Integration Testing (End-to-End Codec)

Scenario Entry point Pass criterion
I-frame only encode → decode Phase 6 pipeline PSNR ≥ 35 dB on 352×288 CIF test sequence, bit-exact decoder reconstruction matches encoder recon
P-frame GOP (IBBP pattern) Phase 11 pipeline I2: encoder recon buffer == decoder frame buffer at every frame, no drift over 100 frames
QP sweep 0..51 vc-encoder + vc-decoder I10: PSNR monotonically non-decreasing as QP decreases for fixed source
CAVLC-coded stream vc-encoder (CAVLC mode) parse(encode(seq)) == seq; PSNR within 0.1 dB of reference path
CABAC-coded stream vc-encoder (CABAC mode) same; verify context models reset at slice boundaries
Slice boundary robustness inject slice splits mid-frame decoder reconstructs identical frames regardless of slice partitioning
Sub-pel MC correctness vc-inter compare quarter-pel prediction blocks against reference JM or hand-computed bilinear values

Differential / Reference-Implementation Testing

  • Reference: JM (H.264/AVC reference software) decode of streams produced by vc-encoder.
  • Ingest vc-encoder YUV output and JM-decoded YUV for identical source; assert per-pixel difference is zero (bit-exact agreement, not just PSNR).
  • Differentials run on ForBIG, Foreman, BQMall CIF/QCIF sequences from the H.264 conformance test set.
  • For streams produced by JM encoder: vc-decoder output vs. JM decoder output, diff must be all-zero.
  • Capture differential failures as regression fixtures; store compressed bitstream + expected YUV md5 in tests/conformance/.

Property-Based Testing (proptest)

Property Generator Assertion
Color roundtrip any::<(u8,u8,u8)>() max(
Transform invertibility array::<i16, 16>() in −2048..2047 inverse(forward(b)) == b
Quant error bound (i32, Qp) `
Exp-Golomb roundtrip 0u32..=(1<<24) read_ue(write_ue(n)) == n
CAVLC coeff roundtrip Vec<i16> length 1..16 decode(encode(v)) == v
CABAC symbol roundtrip Vec<u8> length 1..512 decode(encode(s)) == s
PSNR monotonicity (Frame, qp_hi, qp_lo) qp_hi > qp_lo psnr(encode_decode(f, qp_lo)) ≥ psnr(encode_decode(f, qp_hi))
MV in-bounds (MotionVector, Frame) reconstructed MC block coords clamped/extended within valid range

Fuzz Testing

  • Entry: fuzz/fuzz_targets/parse_bitstream.rs — raw byte input into vc-bitstream NAL parser, then vc-decoder.
  • Invariant: no panic, no OOB access; errors return Err(...) only.
  • Tools: cargo-fuzz (libFuzzer), honggfuzz as secondary.
  • Corpus seed: valid bitstreams from integration tests + malformed truncated variants.
  • Coverage gate: ≥ 85% line coverage on vc-bitstream and vc-entropy parser paths.
  • Regression: any crash-causing input committed to fuzz/corpus/ as a non-regression fixture.
  • CI schedule: fuzz runs 10 min per PR merge on branch, 60 min nightly.

Determinism / Deterministic-Replay Testing

  • I1: vc-decoder invoked twice on identical bitstream → identical output frames; md5(frame_buffer) equality asserted.
  • Deterministic-replay: serialize encoder state (rng seed, QP history, DPB snapshot) at frame 50, reload, continue; output frames 51..100 must be identical to original run.
  • Thread-determinism: run encoder with 1, 2, 4 threads (when MT added); output identical.

Conformance Validation

  • Decode all streams from ITU-T H.264 conformance bitstream suite (JVT-AVC_V1) where the subset of features implemented applies (intra, single-ref inter, CAVLC/CABAC, deblocking).
  • Compare decoded YUV against reference MD5 checksums from the conformance suite documentation.
  • Phase 14 gate: zero conformance failures for supported feature subset.

Compatibility Testing

  • Verify vc-encoder output is playable by: FFmpeg (libavcodec h264 decoder), VLC, GStreamer (vaapih264dec disabled, software only).
  • Test container: raw Annex B byte stream (.264), then wrapped in MPEG-TS via ffmpeg for round-trip.

Regression and Performance-Regression Testing

  • Regression suite: tests/regression/ — each entry is a .264 bitstream + .yuv.md5; any future commit that changes output triggers a gate failure.
  • Performance-regression: criterion benchmarks committed to repo; CI asserts no phase regresses > 10% wall time vs. main branch baseline, using critcmp.

Soak and Fault-Injection Testing

  • Soak: encode/decode 10 000-frame stream at 1080p; assert no memory growth (monitor RSS via /proc/self/status or jemalloc stats), no DPB leak, PSNR stable.
  • Fault injection: truncate bitstream at random byte offset; decoder must return Err within 1 ms, never hang.
  • Slice-drop: drop random NAL units; decoder must not panic, may output corrupted frames.

Dependency Auditing and Supply-Chain

Tool Trigger Gate

Version Stamping and Artifact Signing

  • Version: 0.PHASE.PATCH — PHASE tracks the last complete phase (1..14); PATCH increments for bug-fixes within a phase.
  • build.rs in vc-cli: embeds git rev, build date, rustc --version into the binary via CARGO_PKG_VERSION + env! macros.
  • Release binaries signed with sigstore cosign; signature bundle stored alongside artifact in GitHub Release.
  • SBOM: cargo cyclonedx generates CycloneDX JSON; attached to every release.

Binary Provenance

  • SLSA level 2: provenance attestation generated by slsa-github-generator for each stable release.
